Abstract: Large-scale news corpora support a wide range of research in Computational Social Science and NLP, yet access remains constrained: commercial archives impose prohibitive costs and licensing restrictions, while open alternatives like Common Crawl's CC-News require terabyte-scale storage and computationally intensive processing. We present Infini-News, a retrieval toolkit and index for the entire CC-News archive from August 2016 to the latest available snapshot. Our contributions are threefold. First, we extract, clean the text, and parse the structured metadata of over 1.35B articles. Second, we enrich the corpus with language detection using three frontier language classifiers (GlotLID, lingua, and CommonLingua), and with multi-source geographic attribution that resolves a country of origin for 83.4% of articles across 222 countries. Third, we construct Infini-gram indexes: suffix-array structures that let researchers search the full archive for arbitrary text patterns in sub-second time. Together, these resources lower the barrier to longitudinal, cross-national media research.

</details>


### [78] [Presupposition and Reasoning in Conditionals: A Theory-Based Study of Humans and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18352)
*Tara Azin,Yongan Yu,Raj Singh,Olessia Jouravlev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过对比人类与LLM在条件句预设投射上的判断，发现LLM的表现在于表面模式匹配而非真正的语用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件句中的预设投射是意义和语用理论的核心问题，但大语言模型在这一领域尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 通过平行行为研究，收集120名人类参与者和4个LLM在规范化的条件句数据集上的似然评分，并采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估模型推理。

Result: 人类判断综合了概率和语用线索，而LLM与人类模式的对齐程度不一；评分匹配人类的模型往往缺乏连贯的语用推理，而推理能力强的模型反而不太像人类。

Conclusion: LLM在预设投射任务上的表现可能源于表面模式匹配而非真正的语用能力，凸显了基于语言学理论的基准测试对于人机比较的重要性。

Abstract: Presupposition projection in conditionals is central to theories of meaning and pragmatics, yet it remains largely unevaluated in large language models. We address this gap through a parallel behavioral study comparing human judgments and LLM predictions on a normed dataset of conditional sentences that controls the relation between the antecedent and the projected presupposition. We collect likelihood ratings from 120 participants and four LLMs under matched contextual conditions. Results show that humans integrate probabilistic and pragmatic cues in their judgment, whereas LLMs show variable alignment with human patterns. Using a linguistically motivated checklist within an LLM-as-a-Judge framework, we further evaluate model reasoning. We observe models that best match human ratings often lack coherent pragmatic reasoning, while models with stronger reasoning produce less human-like judgments. These findings suggest that LLMs' performance on such tasks may result from surface pattern matching rather than pragmatic competence. Our findings highlight the importance of benchmarks grounded in linguistic theory for comparing humans and models.

</details>


### [79] [SkillsVote: Lifecycle Governance of Agent Skills from Collection, Recommendation to Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18401)
*Hongyi Liu,Haoyan Yang,Tao Jiang,Bo Tang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillsVote是一个生命周期治理框架，用于管理LLM智能体的技能（可执行脚本与不可执行指南的结合），通过对百万级开源语料库进行画像、合成可验证任务、结构化技能库搜索、轨迹分解与证据门控更新，实现技能从收集、推荐到演化的全流程管控。离线进化使GPT-5.2在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升7.9 pp，在线进化使SWE-Bench Pro提升2.6 pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体产生的轨迹包含可重用的经验，但原始轨迹噪声大且难以管理。现有开放技能生态系统存在冗余、不均衡、环境敏感的工件，且不加区分的更新会污染未来上下文，亟需一个系统化的技能生命周期治理框架。

Method: SkillsVote框架包含：1）对百万级开源语料库进行环境需求、质量和可验证性画像；2）为可验证技能合成任务；3）执行前进行智能体库搜索，暴露结构化技能库中的指令性上下文；4）执行后将轨迹分解为技能关联的子任务，将结果归因于技能使用、智能体探索、环境和结果信号；5）只允许成功的可重用发现通过证据门控更新进入库中。

Result: 离线进化使GPT-5.2在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升最多7.9个百分点；在线进化使SWE-Bench Pro提升最多2.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过系统控制曝光、归因和保存，受治理的外部技能库可以在不更新模型的情况下改进冻结的智能体。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents leave traces that could become reusable experience, but raw trajectories are noisy and hard to govern. We treat Agent Skills as an experience schema that couples executable scripts, with non-executable guidance on procedures. Yet open skill ecosystems contain redundant, uneven, environment-sensitive artifacts, and indiscriminate updates can pollute future context. We present SkillsVote, a lifecycle-governance framework for Agent Skills from collection and recommendation to evolution. SkillsVote profiles a million-scale open-source corpus for environment requirements, quality, and verifiability, then synthesizes tasks for verifiable skills. Before execution, SkillsVote performs agentic library search over structured skill library to expose instructional skill context. After execution, it decomposes trajectories into skill-linked subtasks, attributes outcomes to skill use, agent exploration, environment, and result signals, and admits only successful reusable discoveries to evidence-gated updates. In our evaluation, offline evolution improves GPT-5.2 on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by up to 7.9 pp, while online evolution improves SWE-Bench Pro by up to 2.6 pp. Overall, governed external skill libraries can improve frozen agents without model updates when systems control exposure, credit, and preservation.

</details>


### [80] [From BERT to T5: A Study of Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18462)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本报告研究了两种预训练模型（BERT和T5）在命名实体识别任务上的微调表现，分别在7类标签和3类简化标签方案下进行了实验，并通过超参数消融研究和错误分析对比了两者的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命名实体识别是NLP应用中的关键预处理步骤，有必要探索不同架构（编码器模型与序列到序列模型）在该任务上的表现差异，为实际应用提供参考。

Method: 采用BERT（编码器模型+分类头）和T5（序列到序列模型+少样本提示）两种架构，在7类和3类标签方案下进行微调；BERT使用加权交叉熵损失，T5采用两种验证策略，并进行了超参数消融实验。

Result: 通过多种性能指标对比了两模型的序列标注能力，并提供了BERT常见错误的分析见解。

Conclusion: 该工作为两种架构在NER任务上的能力对比提供了有价值的分析，为后续实际应用场景奠定了基础。

Abstract: Named entity recognition (NER) has been one of the essential preliminary steps in modern NLP applications. This report focuses on implementing the NER task on finetuning two pretrained models: (i) an encoder-only model (BERT) with a simple classification head, and (ii) a sequence-to-sequence model (T5) with few-shot prompts. Under the original 7-class tag and 3-class simplified tag schemes, BERT is applied a weighted cross-entropy for training loss, and T5 is fine-tuned with two validation strategies. It also conducted an ablation study with different hyperparameters. Moreover, the related analysis provides valuable insights into common errors in BERT and the two models' performance. Based on a bunch of performance metrics, this report aims to compare the above two architectures and explore their abilities in the sequence labelling task, laying the groundwork for further practical use cases.

</details>


### [81] [Vector RAG vs LLM-Compiled Wiki: A Preregistered Comparison on a Small Multi-Domain Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18490)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown维基两种系统在小规模研究语料上的问答效果，发现维基在跨论文连接和引证支持上更优，但成本更高；RAG在单事实查找上表现更好；两种架构在证据组织、引证支持和成本三个维度上各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在小规模研究语料上，哪种检索增强系统（单轮向量RAG vs. LLM编译的Markdown维基）能更好地帮助大模型回答问题，尤其是在跨论文综合和引证准确性方面。

Method: 预注册实验：两种系统（单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown维基）回答相同13个问题（基于24篇论文），使用相同的答案生成模型，由盲审LLM评委评分，并进行成本分析和引证级别检查。

Result: 维基在跨论文连接上显著更优，但组织优势经评委调整后不显著；RAG在单事实查找上满足预注册标准；维基查询端成本远高于RAG，未能通过低成本查询回收构建成本；引证检查显示维基在逐条引证支持上更好，而RAG在整体可靠性评分上更优；分解式RAG变体恢复了维基在跨论文综合上的优势，但未能恢复引证支持优势。

Conclusion: 基于研究的综合问答并非单一能力，不同架构在证据组织、引证支持和运行成本三个维度上表现各异，没有一种架构在所有维度上同时最优。

Abstract: We preregistered a comparison of two ways to help an LLM answer questions over a small research corpus: a single-round Vector RAG system and an LLM-compiled markdown wiki. Both systems answered the same 13 questions over 24 papers using the same answer-generating model, and their answers were scored by blinded LLM judges.
  The wiki scored much better at connecting findings across papers, but its advantage in answer organization was not strong after judge adjustment. RAG met the preregistered test for single-fact lookup questions. The clean query-side cost result went against the expected wiki advantage: under the tested setup, the wiki used far more query tokens than RAG, so it could not recover any upfront build cost through cheaper queries.
  Two exploratory analyses changed how we interpret the result. First, claim-level citation checking favored the wiki: its cited pages more often supported the exact claims being made, even though RAG scored better on the overall groundedness rubric. Second, a decomposition-based RAG variant recovered most of the wiki's advantage on cross-paper synthesis at lower LLM-token cost, but it did not recover the wiki advantage in claim-by-claim citation support.
  The main conclusion is that grounded research synthesis is not a single capability. Systems can differ in how well they organize evidence, how well their citations support each claim, and how much they cost to run. In this study, no architecture was best on all three.

</details>


### [82] [Implicit Hierarchical GRPO: Decoupling Tool Invocation from Execution for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18500)
*Li Wang,Xiaohan Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Zipeng Zhang,Jinyang Wu,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将工具调用与执行解耦的延迟执行策略（IH-GRPO），通过层级控制框架提升大语言模型在工具集成推理（TIR）中的性能，在多个数学推理基准上取得1.87%-2.53%的绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将工具调用与立即执行紧密耦合，这会打断LLM的推理连贯性并限制其表达能力，最终损害推理性能。

Method: 首次正式提出将工具调用与执行解耦的问题，引入带显式控制的延迟执行策略；提出层级控制框架，通过理论推导的替代损失使隐式层级策略学习显式层级策略的行为，最终提出IH-GRPO算法。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上，六个域外数学推理基准上分别获得1.87%、2.16%、2.53%的绝对提升，且在其他领域也有一致性能增益。

Conclusion: 解耦工具调用与执行并引入延迟执行策略能有效提升LLM工具集成推理的性能，IH-GRPO算法在多个模型规模和领域上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly leveraged tool invocation to enhance their reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches typically tightly couple tool invocation with immediate execution. Such immediate tool interaction may disrupt the reasoning coherence of LLMs and constrain their expressivity, ultimately degrading reasoning performance. To this end, for the first time, we propose and formalize the problem of decoupling tool invocation from execution during reasoning, and introduce delayed execution with explicit control to enhance tool-integrated reasoning (TIR). Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical control framework and theoretically derive a surrogate loss that enables an implicitly hierarchical policy to learn behavior equivalent to that of an explicit hierarchical policy, leading to the proposed IH-GRPO algorithm. Extensive experiments on IH-GRPO achieve absolute improvements of 1.87\%, 2.16\%, and 2.53\% on Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B across six out-of-domain mathematical reasoning benchmarks over the strongest baseline method, while also yielding consistent performance gains in other domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/IH-GRPO-01.

</details>


### [83] [Ancient Greek to Modern Greek Machine Translation: A Novel Benchmark and Fine-Tuning Experiments on LLMs and NMT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18504)
*Spyridon Mavromatis,Sokratis Sofianopoulos,Prokopis Prokopidis,Maria Giagkou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对古希腊语到现代希腊语的机器翻译低资源问题，构建了包含13.2万句对的AG-MG平行语料库，提出了一种结合网页抓取、多阶段句对齐（VecAlign+微调LaBSE）和LLM纠错（Gemini 2.5 Flash）的语料构建流程，并首次对多种现代MT模型（NLLB、M2M100、Llama-Krikri-8B）进行基准测试，发现全参数微调Llama-Krikri-8B取得最高BLEU 13.16，QLoRA适配的M2M100-1.2B增益最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古希腊语到现代希腊语的机器翻译面临低资源困境，缺乏大规模、高质量的双语平行数据，制约了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 1) 构建AG-MG平行语料库，包含132,481句对，来源于文学、历史和圣经文本；2) 提出语料构建流程：网页抓取+多阶段句对齐（VecAlign结合微调后的LaBSE嵌入）+LLM纠错（Gemini 2.5 Flash）；3) 评估三种微调策略（NLLB、M2M100、Llama-Krikri-8B）在NMT模型上的表现。

Result: 微调相比基线模型最高提升+10.3 BLEU；全参数微调Llama-Krikri-8B取得最佳性能（BLEU 13.16）；QLoRA适配的M2M100-1.2B相对增益最大且结果极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的数据集和模型对希腊语NLP领域做出了重要贡献，为低资源古希腊语-现代希腊语翻译提供了有效基线和方法参考。

Abstract: Machine Translation (MT) for Ancient Greek (AG) to Modern Greek (MG) is a low-resource task, constrained by the lack of large-scale, high-quality parallel data. We address this gap by introducing the AG-MG Parallel Corpus, a new resource containing 132,481 sentence-aligned pairs derived from literary, historical, and biblical texts. We present a novel corpus creation pipeline that combines web-scraped, excerpt-level data with a multi-stage sentence-level alignment, and refinement process. Our method uses VecAlign with LaBSE embeddings, which we first fine-tune on a manually-aligned AG-MG subset, followed by an LLM-based error/misalignment correction phase using Gemini 2.5 Flash to ensure high alignment quality. Furthermore, we provide the first comprehensive benchmark of modern MT models on this task, evaluating three fine-tuning strategies across NMT models (NLLB, M2M100) and a Greek LLM (Llama-Krikri-8B). Our experiments show that fine-tuning yields significant improvements over base models, increasing performance by up to +10.3 BLEU points. Specifically, full-parameter fine-tuning of Llama-Krikri-8B achieves the highest overall performance with a BLEU score of 13.16, while the QLoRA-adapted M2M100-1.2B model demonstrates the largest relative gains and highly competitive results. Our dataset and models represent a significant contribution to Greek NLP.

</details>


### [84] [Easier to Judge than to Find: Predicting In-Context Learning Success for Demonstration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18512)
*Haochun Wang,Chaofen Yang,Jiatong Liu,Jingbo Wang,Zewen Qiang,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSP框架，通过先随机采样评估查询难度，再训练轻量级路由器进行难度预测，并训练难度级别的评估器来筛选示例，在五个分类数据集上取得了最优平均准确率（最高提升3.4%）和高达23倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）对提示中的示例非常敏感，但选择合适的示例成本高昂，因为可能的示例组合空间巨大。作者认为“评估比搜索更容易”，即预测某个查询-示例对是否有效比搜索最优示例更高效。

Method: 提出DiSP（样本-评估）框架：1）通过随机示例试验估算每个训练查询的成功率来分层查询难度；2）训练轻量级路由器从查询本身预测难度；3）为不同难度级别训练专门的评估器。推理时在显式预算下执行“接受即停止”的评估，并在找不到合适上下文时发出诊断风险标签。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-2.5-7B上，使用五个分类数据集进行实验，DiSP取得了最佳平均准确率，比强基线方法高出最多3.4%，同时实现了高达23倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 验证了ICL中“评估比搜索更容易”这一核心假设的有效性，表明通过难度分层和轻量级评估器可以兼顾效果与效率。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is highly sensitive to which demonstrations appear in the prompt, but selecting them is expensive because the space of possible demonstration contexts and combinations is enormous. We argue that demonstration selection is \emph{easier to judge than to find}: predicting whether a specific query--context pair $(q,D)$ will succeed is cheaper and more general than searching for an optimal $D^\star$. Based on this insight, we propose DiSP, a sample-and-judge framework that stratifies queries by difficulty. DiSP runs random demonstration trials to estimate success rate of each training query, trains a lightweight router to predict difficulty from the query, and trains level-specific judges for sampled demonstrations. At inference, DiSP performs stop-on-acceptance judging under an explicit budget, emitting diagnostic risk tags when no suitable context is found. Across five classification datasets with Llama~3--8B and Qwen~2.5--7B, DiSP achieves the best average accuracy, improving over strong learned selection baselines by up to 3.4\%, while achieving up to $23\times$ end-to-end wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [85] [STT-Arena: A More Realistic Environment for Tool-Using with Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18548)
*Tingfeng Hui,Hao Xu,Pengyu Zhu,Hongsheng Xin,Kun Zhan,Sen Su,Chunxiao Liu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STT-Arena基准，评估LLM在时空动态变化下的自适应重新规划能力，发现前沿模型准确率不足40%，并通过分析失败模式提出迭代轨迹优化和在线RL方法，训练出4B参数模型超越前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态基准主要关注LLM能否及时检测时间变化，而忽略了自适应重新规划这一互补性挑战，特别是在时空动态场景下的规划能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建包含227个高质量交互任务的STT-Arena基准，涵盖9种时空冲突类型和4个可解性级别；通过注入时空触发器使计划失效，评估模型状态转换检测与重新规划能力；提出迭代轨迹优化技术消除训练数据中的失败模式，结合在线强化学习训练STT-Agent-4B模型。

Result: 前沿模型（包括Claude-4.6-Opus）在STT-Arena上准确率不足40%；识别出三种失败模式：过时状态执行、动态触发器误诊断、缺乏自适应后验证；STT-Agent-4B模型超越所有前沿LLM。

Conclusion: 时空动态推理对当前LLM仍是根本性挑战，通过针对性数据清洗和在线RL训练的小模型可以显著超越大模型，为构建更鲁棒的智能体系统提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in real-world agentic applications must be capable of replanning and adapting when mid-task disruptions invalidate their prior decisions. Existing dynamic benchmarks primarily measure whether LLMs can detect temporal changes in a timely manner, leaving the complementary challenge of adaptive replanning under spatio-temporal dynamics largely unexplored. We introduce STT-Arena (Spatio-Temporal Tool-Use Arena), a benchmark of 227 high-quality interactive tasks spanning nine spatio-temporal conflict types and four solvability levels. Each task is grounded in a realistic, executable environment equipped with injected spatio-temporal triggers that can abruptly invalidate an ongoing plan, forcing the model to detect the state shift and construct a revised execution strategy. Extensive evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that even the SOTA proprietary models, including Claude-4.6-Opus, achieves less than 40\% overall accuracies, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of spatio-temporal dynamic reasoning. Systematic analysis of failure trajectories uncovers three recurring error modes of existing models: Stale-State Execution, Misdiagnosis of Dynamic Triggers, and Missing Post-Adaptation Verification. Guided by these findings, we propose an iterative trajectory refinement technique that eliminates these failure patterns from training data, and combine it with online RL to produce STT-Agent-4B which outperforms frontier LLMs on STT-Arena.

</details>


### [86] [Monitoring the Internal Monologue: Probe Trajectories Reveal Reasoning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18549)
*Maciej Chrabąszcz,Aleksander Szymczyk,Marcin Sendera,Tomasz Trzciński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过探测轨迹（probe trajectories）——即在大推理模型（LRM）的链式思维推理过程中连续监测概念概率的演化——来预测模型未来行为。研究发现，基于完整轨迹的特征（如波动性、趋势和稳态行为）比单点预测更能区分模型未来状态，且最大池化操作可达95%的AUROC，为LRM行为监控提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM的链式思维推理为安全监控提供了新机会，但CoT并不总是忠实于模型的最终输出，这削弱了其作为监控工具的可靠性。因此需要探索能否通过提示和CoT的隐藏表示来预测模型的未来行为。

Method: 在每个生成token处评估一个探针（probe），构建探针轨迹——概念概率在推理过程中的连续演化曲线。提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态行为）来刻画时间动态；比较不同池化操作（平均池化、最后token、最大池化）的效果；使用基于模板的训练数据替代动态生成数据。

Result: 完整轨迹比单点静态预测能更好地区分未来模型行为；最大池化操作可达95% AUROC，而平均池化和最后token方法接近随机水平；模板训练数据与动态生成数据性能接近；轨迹特征编码了任务特定的动态信息，提高了结果可分离性。

Conclusion: 探针轨迹作为监控LRM行为的补充框架是有效的，通过捕捉推理过程中的连续动态信号，能更可靠地预测模型未来行为，且无需昂贵的初始推理和标注流程。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) introduce new opportunities for safety monitoring through their Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, CoT is not always faithful to the model's final output, undermining its reliability as a monitoring tool. To address this, we investigate the hidden representations of LRMs to determine whether future behavior can be predicted from prompt and CoT representations. By evaluating a probe at each generated token, we construct a probe trajectory, the continuous evolution of a concept's probability across the reasoning process. We find that future model behavior is more distinguishable when examined over the full trajectory than from a single static prediction. To characterize these temporal dynamics, we extract signal-processing features that capture volatility, trend, and steady-state behavior, significantly improving the separation of future model states. We also present two methodological insights. First, template-based training data achieves near-parity with dynamically generated model responses, eliminating the need for a costly initial inference and labeling. Second, the choice of pooling operation is critical: average-pooling and last-token methods collapse to near-random performance, while max-pooling achieves up to 95% AUROC and yields stable probe trajectories. Using four datasets and four reasoning models across the domains of safety and mathematics, we demonstrate that trajectory features encode task-specific dynamics that improve outcome separability. These findings establish probe trajectories as a complementary framework for monitoring LRM behavior.
  Warning: This article contains potentially harmful content.

</details>


### [87] [Readers make targeted regressions to plausible errors in reanalysis of "noisy-channel garden-path" sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18563)
*Thomas Hikaru Clark,Roger Levy,Edward Gibson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过眼动实验发现，读者在处理“噪音通道花园路径句”时，会表现出针对性的回视行为——眼睛会移向可能包含错误的文本区域，这与噪音通道处理模型中的后验推理模式一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究读者如何在理解过程中逐步推断语言输入的含义，特别是当句子表面上看似合理但后续出现预期违背时，读者如何通过推断错误的存在而非重构句法结构来理解句子。

Method: 使用眼动追踪技术，研究读者阅读“噪音通道花园路径句”时的动态阅读行为，并与噪音通道处理模型中的后验推理模式进行比较。

Result: 发现读者存在针对性的回视行为，即眼睛会移向可能包含错误的文本区域，这种行为模式与噪音通道处理模型中的后验推理相一致。

Conclusion: 这些发现对噪音通道语言理解理论和阅读动态的信息论解释具有重要意义。

Abstract: A key question in psycholinguistics is how inferences about the meaning of linguistic input unfold incrementally a comprehender's mind. In this work, we study reading dynamics for ``noisy-channel garden-path'' sentences, which temporarily appear well-formed but feature late-appearing violations of expectation that can be resolved not by inferring an alternative syntactic structure, but by inferring the presence of an error. We find evidence for targeted regressions -- eye movements towards regions that are promising loci of possible errors in light of later-arriving information, showing patterns consistent with the posterior inferences of a model of noisy-channel processing with reanalysis. We discuss the implications of these findings for theories of noisy-channel language comprehension and information-theoretic explanations of reading dynamics.

</details>


### [88] [LongMINT: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18565)
*Hyunji Lee,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Joykirat Singh,Zaid Khan,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LongMINT基准，评估现有记忆增强型智能体在高干扰、长时域场景下的表现，发现平均准确率仅27.9%，主要受限于检索和记忆构建能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准聚焦于静态、独立的记忆召回，无法捕捉现实场景中信息动态更新、记忆之间相互干扰的复杂情况。

Method: 构建LongMINT基准，包含长且高度关联的上下文（平均138.8k tokens，最长1.8M tokens）、四个多样域（状态追踪、多轮对话、Wikipedia修订、GitHub提交）以及两类问题（单目标召回和多目标聚合），共15.6k个问答对。

Result: 评估7种系统（包括长上下文LLM、RAG、记忆增强智能体框架），平均准确率仅27.9%，多目标聚合任务表现更差；性能主要受限于检索和记忆构建，且随中间更新次数增加而下降。

Conclusion: 当前记忆增强系统在干扰密集的长时域场景中表现不佳，尤其在需要聚合推理的任务上，亟需改进检索和记忆构建机制。

Abstract: Real-world agents operate over long and evolving horizons, where information is repeatedly updated and may interfere across memories, requiring accurate recall and aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of information. However, existing benchmarks focus on static, independent recall and fail to capture these dynamic interactions between evolving memories. In this paper, we study how current memory-augmented agents perform in realistic, interference-heavy, long-horizon settings across diverse domains and question types. We introduce LongMINT (Long-Horizon Memory under INTerference), a benchmark featuring (1) long, highly interconnected contexts with frequently updated information that induces substantial interference, (2) diverse domains (state tracking, multi-turn dialogue, Wikipedia revisions, and GitHub commits), enabling evaluation of domain generalization, and (3) diverse question types that assess robustness to interference, including (i) single-target recall tasks requiring retrieval of a specific target from long contexts, and (ii) multi-target aggregation tasks requiring reasoning over multiple relevant pieces of information. Overall, LongMINT has 15.6k question-answering pairs over long-horizon contexts averaging 138.8k tokens and extending up to 1.8M tokens per instance. We evaluate 7 representative systems, including vanilla long-context LLMs, RAG, and memory-augmented agent frameworks. Across all systems, we observe consistently low performance (avg. 27.9% accuracy), especially on questions requiring aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence. Our analysis shows that performance is primarily limited by retrieval and memory construction. Furthermore, current memory systems struggle to recall and reason over earlier facts that are later revised or interfered with by subsequent context, with performance degrading as the number of intervening updates increases.

</details>


### [89] [GUT-IS: A Data-Driven Approach to Integrating Constructs and Their Relations in Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18567)
*Maximilian Reinhardt,Jonas Scharfenberger,Burkhardt Funk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合任务适应的文本嵌入与聚类的方法，用于将结构方程模型中的构念整合为统一模型，并通过显式权衡语义纯度与简约性的损失函数选择最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在IS研究中，结构方程模型被广泛使用，但构念定义不一致阻碍了知识的累积发展。

Method: 结合任务适应的文本嵌入与聚类生成候选构念分组，然后使用显式权衡语义纯度与聚类数量的损失函数选择最优解。

Result: 在两个IS领域数据集上对所提方法进行了实证评估与探索。

Conclusion: 该方法通过显式权衡纯度与简约性，能够分析构念分组及其关系如何随优先级变化而改变。

Abstract: Structural equation modeling is widely used in IS research. However, inconsistent construct definitions impede the cumulative development of knowledge. In this work, we present an approach that aims at the integration of structural equation models into a unified model: We use a combination of task-adapted text embeddings and clustering to produce a candidate set of construct groupings. Subsequently, we select the optimal solution using a loss function that explicitly trades off semantic purity and parsimony in the number of clusters. By making this trade-off explicit, our approach allows to analyze how construct groupings and their relations change as one shifts the priority from purity to parsimony. Empirically, we evaluate and explore the proposed methodology on two datasets from the IS domain.

</details>


### [90] [MA$^{2}$P: A Meta-Cognitive Autonomous Intelligent Agents Framework for Complex Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18572)
*Dingyi Zhang,Ziqing Zhuang,Linhai Zhang,Ziyang Gao,Deyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MA²P框架，一种元认知自主智能体架构，通过多智能体协作和元认知配置器来提升复杂说服对话的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前说服对话生成方法存在两个主要问题：一是当说服对象的内部状态不明确时，现有方法只能生成通用或弱相关的回应；二是大语言模型在不同领域的说服表现差异大，知识覆盖不均衡且推理泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出MA²P元认知自主智能体框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 自主多智能体架构，协调感知管理、心理状态推理、策略执行、记忆维护和性能评估；(2) 元认知配置器，在对话开始时从结构化知识库中选择合适的元策略，指导后续推理和规划。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法比基线方法取得了更高的说服成功率。

Conclusion: 通过元认知机制和多智能体协作，MA²P框架有效提升了跨领域说服对话的表现，解决了现有方法在推理泛化和回应针对性方面的不足。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue generation plays a vital role in decision-making, negotiation, counseling, and behavior change, yet it remains a challenging problem. In complex persuasion where the persuadee's internal states are not expressed clearly, the persuader must interpret responses, infer the persuadee's latent mental states (e.g., beliefs and desires), and translate them into targeted, strategy-consistent actions; however, current approaches often produce generic or weakly grounded responses even when such cues are identified. Moreover, although large language models (LLMs) can generate persuasive content, their performance varies substantially across domains due to uneven knowledge coverage and limited reasoning generalization. To address these challenges, we propose MA$^{2}$P, a meta-cognitive autonomous intelligent agent framework for complex persuasion. Specifically, we develop an autonomous multi-agent architecture that coordinates perception management, mental-state inference, strategy execution, memory maintenance, and performance evaluation. To mitigate cross-domain performance variation, we further design a meta-cognitive configurator that selects an appropriate meta-strategy from a structured knowledge base at the outset, thereby guiding subsequent reasoning and planning. Experimental results show that our approach achieves a higher persuasion success rate than baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Language-Switching Triggers Take a Latent Detour Through Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18646)
*Francis Kulumba,Wissam Antoun,Théo Lasnier,Benoît Sagot,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了8B参数自回归语言模型中语言切换后门攻击的内部电路机制，揭示了触发令牌通过三层级计算流程劫持模型输出（英语→法语），并发现关键瓶颈位置及正交潜在编码特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后门攻击对语言模型构成日益严重的安全威胁，但触发器序列如何劫持模型计算的内部机制尚不明确，现有研究缺乏对后门攻击底层计算路径的深入理解。

Method: 在8B参数自回归语言模型中植入语言切换后门（三词拉丁触发词将英语输出重定向为法语），通过电路分析分解为三个阶段：早期层分布式注意力头将触发令牌汇聚到最后位置；中层在正交于自然语言身份方向的子空间中传播信号；最终层MLP将潜在信号转换为法语logits。

Result: 整个电路流经单个位置的序列瓶颈，破坏该位置可完全消除触发器效果但也损害模型能力；正交潜在编码表明基于中间表示语言特征的防御会完全遗漏该触发器。

Conclusion: 揭示了后门攻击在语言模型中的精确神经电路机制，发现了正交子空间编码这一难以被现有防御检测的特性，对设计更有效的后门防御策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on language models pose a growing security concern, yet the internal mechanisms by which a trigger sequence hijacks model computations remain poorly understood. We identify a circuit underlying a language-switching backdoor in an 8B-parameter autoregressive language model, where a three-word Latin trigger (nine tokens) redirects English output to French. We decompose the circuit into three phases: (1) distributed attention heads at early layers compose the trigger tokens into the last sequence position; (2) the resulting signal propagates through mid-layers in a subspace orthogonal to the model's natural language-identity direction; (3) the MLP at the final layer converts this latent signal into French logits. The entire circuit flows through a serial bottleneck at a single position: corrupting that position at any layer entirely mitigate the trigger but also hinder the model's capabilities. The orthogonal latent encoding suggests that defenses that search for language-like signals in intermediate representations would miss this trigger entirely.

</details>


### [92] [EnvFactory: Scaling Tool-Use Agents via Executable Environments Synthesis and Robust RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18703)
*Minrui Xu,Zilin Wang,Mengyi DENG,Zhiwei Li,Zhicheng Yang,Xiao Zhu,Yinhong Liu,Boyu Zhu,Baiyu Huang,Chao Chen,Heyuan Deng,Fei Mi,Lifeng Shang,Xingshan Zeng,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EnvFactory是一个全自动框架，通过自主构建可执行工具环境并合成自然的多轮轨迹，解决了Agentic RL中环境缺失和数据稀缺两大瓶颈，仅用85个环境就显著提升了LLM的工具使用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖昂贵的真实API、易产生幻觉的LLM模拟器或仅支持单轮交互的合成环境，且合成轨迹常过于明确、缺乏隐式推理，导致Agentic RL训练效果不佳。

Method: EnvFactory通过自主探索并验证真实资源中的可执行工具环境，结合拓扑感知采样和校准优化技术，合成包含隐式意图的自然多轮轨迹。

Result: 使用仅85个环境（远少于以往工作的5倍），生成了2,575条SFT和RL轨迹，在BFCLv3上提升Qwen3系列模型最多+15%，MCP-Atlas上+8.6%，对话基准上+6%。

Conclusion: EnvFactory通过完全自动化环境构建和轨迹合成，为Agentic RL提供了可扩展、可扩展且稳健的基础，展示了高质量环境比大规模环境更有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Equipping LLMs with tool-use capabilities via Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) is bottlenecked by two challenges: the lack of scalable, robust execution environments and the scarcity of realistic training data that captures implicit human reasoning. Existing approaches depend on costly real-world APIs, hallucination-prone LLM simulators, or synthetic environments that are often single-turn or depend on pre-collected documents. Moreover, synthetic trajectories are frequently over-specified, resembling instruction sequences rather than natural human intents, reducing their effectiveness for RL training. We introduce EnvFactory, a fully automated framework that addresses both challenges. EnvFactory autonomously explores and verifies stateful, executable tool environments from authentic resources, and synthesizes natural multi-turn trajectories through topology-aware sampling and calibrated refinement, producing grounded queries with implicit intents. Using only 85 verified environments across 7 domains, EnvFactory generates 2,575 SFT and RL trajectories. Despite using significantly fewer environments than prior work, which are often 5 times more, EnvFactory achieves superior training efficiency and downstream performance, improving Qwen3-series models by up to +15% on BFCLv3, +8.6% on MCP-Atlas, and +6% on conversational benchmarks including $τ^2$-Bench and VitaBench. By fully automating both environment construction and trajectory synthesis, EnvFactory provides a scalable, extensible, and robust foundation for Agentic RL.

</details>


### [93] [Predictable Confabulations: Factual Recall by LLMs Scales with Model Size and Topic Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18732)
*Matthew L. Smith,Jonathan P. Shock,Samuel T. Segun,Iyiola E. Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，大型语言模型的事实回忆质量遵循一个S型曲线，该曲线由模型参数数量与训练数据中主题表示的对数线性组合决定，这一发现解释了60-94%的模型性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律主要描述模型整体性能，但缺乏将事实回忆能力与模型规模和训练数据组成联系起来的具体缩放规律。

Method: 对38个模型在超过8,900条学术参考文献上使用自动化参考验证系统进行评估，分析模型参数量和训练数据中主题表示对回忆质量的联合影响。

Result: 回忆质量遵循以模型参数量和主题表示的对数线性组合为变量的S型曲线；这两个变量解释了16个密集模型60%的方差，在单个模型家族内解释力提升至74-94%。

Conclusion: 该模式符合基于叠加原理的解释：回忆由信噪比门控，信号强度随概念频率缩放，噪声基底随模型容量缩放。

Abstract: While scaling laws govern aggregate large language model performance, no scaling law has linked factual recall to both model size and training-data composition. We evaluated 38 models on over 8,900 scholarly references evaluated by an automated reference verification system. Recall quality follows a sigmoid in the log-linear combination of model parameter count and topic representation in training data. These two variables alone explain 60% of the variance across 16 dense models from four families, rising to 74-94% within individual families. The form matches a superposition-inspired account in which recall is gated by a signal-to-noise ratio: signal strength scales with concept frequency and the noise floor with model capacity.

</details>


### [94] [Code as Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18747)
*Xuying Ning,Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Zihao Li,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Mengting Ai,Zhining Liu,Ting-Wei Li,Lingjie Chen,Yanjun Zhao,Ke Yang,Bingxuan Li,Cheng Qian,Gaotang Li,Xiao Lin,Zhichen Zeng,Ruizhong Qiu,Sirui Chen,Yifan Sun,Xiyuan Yang,Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Chenyuan Yang,Dylan Zhang,Liri Fang,Zikun Cui,Yang Cao,Pan Chen,Dorothy Sun,Ren Chen,Mahesh Srinivasan,Nipun Mathur,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Pan Lu,Lingming Zhang,Tong Zhang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"代码即智能体框架"的统一视角，认为代码不仅是目标输出，更是智能体推理、行动、环境建模和执行验证的基础设施。围绕框架接口、框架机制和框架扩展三个层次，系统梳理了相关方法与应用，并讨论了评估、验证、多智能体协调等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在代码理解和生成方面展现强大能力，代码在智能体系统中的作用正在发生根本性转变——从单纯的输出目标演变为智能体运作的基础设施。然而，现有研究缺乏对这一转变的系统性认知框架，亟需统一视角来理解代码如何在智能体系统中充当推理、行动和环境建模的核心载体。

Method: 构建三层分析框架：(1) 框架接口层：代码连接智能体与推理、行动、环境建模的方式；(2) 框架机制层：支持长周期执行的规划、记忆、工具使用，以及反馈驱动的控制与优化；(3) 框架扩展层：从单智能体扩展到多智能体系统，共享代码制品支持协调、审查与验证。在此基础上总结代表性方法和实际应用。

Result: 识别出代码作为智能体框架在编码助手、GUI/OS自动化、具身智能、科学发现、个性化推荐、DevOps和企业工作流等多个领域的应用模式，并揭示了评估方法、不完全反馈下的验证、无回归改进、多智能体一致状态、人工监督和多模态环境扩展等关键开放挑战。

Conclusion: 通过将代码置于智能体系统的核心框架位置，本文为构建可执行、可验证、有状态的AI智能体系统提供了统一的研究路线图，强调了代码作为智能体基础设施的范式转变。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding and generating code, from competitive programming to repository-level software engineering. In emerging agentic systems, code is no longer only a target output. It increasingly serves as an operational substrate for agent reasoning, acting, environment modeling, and execution-based verification. We frame this shift through the lens of agent harnesses and introduce code as agent harness: a unified view that centers code as the basis for agent infrastructure. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around three connected layers. First, we study the harness interface, where code connects agents to reasoning, action, and environment modeling. Second, we examine harness mechanisms: planning, memory, and tool use for long-horizon execution, together with feedback-driven control and optimization that make harness reliable and adaptive. Third, we discuss scaling the harness from single-agent systems to multi-agent settings, where shared code artifacts support multi-agent coordination, review, and verification. Across these layers, we summarize representative methods and practical applications of code as agent harness, spanning coding assistants, GUI/OS automation, embodied agents, scientific discovery, personalization and recommendation, DevOps, and enterprise workflows. We further outline open challenges for harness engineering, including evaluation beyond final task success, verification under incomplete feedback, regression-free harness improvement, consistent shared state across multiple agents, human oversight for safety-critical actions, and extensions to multimodal environments. By centering code as the harness of agentic AI, this survey provides a unified roadmap toward executable, verifiable, and stateful AI agent systems.

</details>


### [95] [DashAttention: Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18753)
*Yuxiang Huang,Nuno M. T. Gonçalves,Federico Alvetreti,Lei Li,Xu Han,Edoardo M. Ponti,André F. T. Martins,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DashAttention提出了一种可微分且自适应的稀疏分层注意力机制，使用α-entmax变换动态选择可变数量的KV块，实现端到端可微分，在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的精度，且推理速度超过FlashAttention-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层注意力方法（如NSA、InfLLMv2）使用固定的top-k操作选择KV块，无法根据查询自适应调整相关token数量，且阻碍了稀疏阶段与密集阶段之间的梯度流动。

Method: 利用自适应的稀疏α-entmax变换替代top-k操作，在第一阶段动态选择可变数量的KV块，为第二阶段的softmax注意力提供先验，保持整个层次结构的可微分性。

Result: 在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的精度；在高度稀疏场景下，Pareto前沿优于NSA和InfLLMv2；基于Triton的高效实现在推理时速度超过FlashAttention-3。

Conclusion: DashAttention提供了一种经济有效的长上下文建模策略，兼具可微分性、自适应性和非扩散性，适合大规模语言模型的长序列处理。

Abstract: Current hierarchical attention methods, such as NSA and InfLLMv2, select the top-k relevant key-value (KV) blocks based on coarse attention scores and subsequently apply fine-grained softmax attention on the selected tokens. However, the top-k operation assumes the number of relevant tokens for any query is fixed and it precludes the gradient flow between the sparse and dense stages. In this work, we propose DashAttention (Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention), which leverages the adaptively sparse $α$-entmax transformation to select a variable number of blocks according to the current query in the first stage. This in turn provides a prior for the second-stage softmax attention, keeping the entire hierarchy fully differentiable. Contrary to other hierarchical attention methods, we show that DashAttention is non-dispersive, translating to better long-context modeling ability. Experiments with large language models (LLMs) show that DashAttention achieves comparable accuracy as full attention with 75% sparsity and a better Pareto frontier than NSA and InfLLMv2, especially in high-sparsity regimes. We also provide an efficient, GPU-aware implementation of DashAttention in Triton, which achieves a speedup of up to over FlashAttention-3 at inference time. Overall, DashAttention offers a cost-effective strategy to model long contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Systematic Optimization of Real-Time Diffusion Model Inference on Apple M3 Ultra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16259)
*Yoichi Ochiai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地探索了在Apple M3 Ultra上实现实时图像生成（22.7 FPS）的优化方法，揭示了Apple Silicon与NVIDIA GPU截然不同的优化特性，最终通过CoreML转换+蒸馏模型+多线程流水线实现了高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非CUDA平台（如Apple Silicon）的扩散模型系统优化研究极度缺乏，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 在Apple M3 Ultra上进行了10个阶段的全面优化实验，包括CoreML转换、量化、Token Merging、Neural Engine利用、紧凑模型探索、帧插值、kNN搜索合成、pix2pix-turbo、光流跳帧和知识蒸馏。

Result: 结合蒸馏模型SDXS-512的CoreML转换与3线程相机流水线，在512x512分辨率下实现了22.7 FPS的实时相机img2img转换。

Conclusion: CUDA平台的优化经验在Apple Silicon统一内存架构上不直接适用，量化无加速、并行推理无效、Neural Engine不适合大模型等特性揭示了截然不同的优化格局，并提供了实用指南。

Abstract: While real-time image generation using diffusion models has advanced rapidly on NVIDIA GPUs, systematic optimization research on non-CUDA platforms such as Apple Silicon remains extremely limited. In this study, we conducted comprehensive optimization experiments across 10 phases targeting the Apple M3 Ultra (60-core GPU, 512 GB unified memory) with the goal of achieving real-time camera img2img transformation. We explored a wide range of techniques including CoreML conversion, quantization, Token Merging, Neural Engine utilization, compact model exploration, frame interpolation, kNN search-based synthesis, pix2pix-turbo, optical flow frame skipping, and knowledge distillation, quantitatively evaluating the effectiveness of each approach. Ultimately, by combining CoreML conversion of the distillation-specialized model SDXS-512 with a 3-thread camera pipeline, we achieved real-time camera img2img transformation at 22.7 FPS at 512x512 resolution. The primary contribution of this work is the systematic demonstration that optimization insights established for CUDA are not necessarily effective on Apple Silicon's unified memory architecture. We reveal an optimization landscape fundamentally different from that of NVIDIA GPUs -- including the absence of speedup from quantization, the ineffectiveness of parallel inference, and the unsuitability of the Neural Engine for large-scale models -- and provide practical guidelines for diffusion model inference on Apple Silicon.

</details>


### [97] [Mirror Descent-Type Algorithms for the Variational Inequality Problem with Functional Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16262)
*Mohammad S. Alkousa,Fedor S. Stonyakin,Belal A. Alashqar,Seydamet S. Ablaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带功能性不等式约束的变分不等式问题，提出了多种基于镜像下降法的算法，通过在生产步骤与非生产步骤之间切换来处理约束，实现了最优收敛率，并针对多约束情况提出了改进版本以节省计算时间，同时将方法扩展到了δ-单调算子情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分不等式在机器学习（如GAN、强化学习、对抗训练等）中具有重要作用，但现有研究对带功能性不等式约束的变分不等式问题关注不足，因此本文旨在解决这类约束变分不等式问题。

Method: 提出镜像下降类算法，根据迭代点处功能性约束的取值在生产步骤和非生产步骤之间切换；设计了多种步长规则和停止准则；并针对多约束情况提出改进：在生产步骤中计算所有约束，但仅考虑第一个违反可行性的约束，以节省运行时间。

Result: 在算子有界且单调、功能性约束为Lipschitz凸函数的条件下，证明了所提算法具有最优收敛率；此外，将分析扩展到δ-单调算子，可应用于目标函数次梯度信息不完全可得的约束最小化问题。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在理论上达到最优收敛率，改进版本在多约束场景下能有效节省计算时间，数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Abstract: Variational inequalities play a key role in machine learning research, such as generative adversarial networks, reinforcement learning, adversarial training, and generative models. This paper is devoted to the constrained variational inequality problems with functional constraints (inequality-type constraints). We propose some mirror descent-type algorithms that switch between productive and non-productive steps depending on the values of the functional constraints at iterations, with many different step size rules and stopping criteria. We analyze the proposed algorithms and prove their optimal convergence rate to achieve a solution with desired accuracy, for problems with bounded and monotone operators and Lipschitz convex functional constraints. In addition, we propose a modification of the proposed algorithms by considering each functional constraint in the calculation when we have a productive step, as well as the first constraint that violates the feasibility. This modification can save the running time of algorithms when we have many functional constraints. In addition, we provide an analysis of the proposed algorithms for $δ$-monotone operators, allowing us to apply the proposed algorithms, as a special case, to constrained minimization problems when we do not have access to the exact information about the subgradient of the objective function. Numerical experiments that illustrate the work and performance of the proposed algorithms are also given.

</details>


### [98] [Reducing Credit Assignment Variance via Counterfactual Reasoning Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16302)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Yeling Peng,Youwei Wang,Guoxiong Zhou,Zijian Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐式行为策略优化（IBPO），通过反事实比较的信用分配框架，将稀疏终端奖励转化为步骤级学习信号，提升LLM在多步推理中的训练稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法在大语言模型多步推理中依赖稀疏的终端奖励，导致信用分配困难、梯度方差大、训练不稳定，模型难以持续提升。

Method: 基于反事实比较的信用分配框架，对同一输入采样多条推理轨迹，利用轨迹间差异隐式近似替代决策，构建隐式过程级优势估计器，将稀疏终端奖励转化为步骤敏感的学习信号，提出IBPO算法。

Result: 在数学和代码推理基准上，IBPO显著提升了训练稳定性和性能上限。

Conclusion: IBPO为解锁大语言模型性能潜力提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-step reasoning with large language models (LLMs) often relies on sparse terminal rewards, leading to poor credit assignment conditions where the final feedback is evenly propagated across all intermediate decisions. This results in high gradient variance, unstable training, and numerous ineffective updates, ultimately causing the model to fail and preventing sustained improvement. We introduce a counterfactual comparison-based credit assignment framework, which samples multiple reasoning trajectories under the same input. By treating their differences as an implicit approximation of alternative decisions, we construct an implicit process-level advantage estimator that transforms sparse terminal rewards into step-sensitive learning signals. Based on this, we propose Implicit Behavior Policy Optimization (IBPO), which significantly improves training stability and performance upper bounds on mathematical and code reasoning benchmarks, pointing to a promising direction for unlocking the performance potential of LLMs.

</details>


### [99] [When Actions Disappear: Adversarial Action Removal in Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16312)
*Arahan Kujur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究自对弈强化学习中的对抗性动作掩码攻击，即攻击者选择性移除受害者的合法动作，导致比随机掩码和扰动基线更严重的性能下降，且攻击具有跨算法迁移性、自对弈放大效应，并提出了两种度量指标（CAC_w和CAC_v）来表征攻击机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自对弈强化学习中动作可用性作为一个独立的鲁棒性攻击面，研究攻击者通过选择性移除合法动作（而非扰动观察或动作）对智能体造成的破坏程度。

Method: 采用对抗性动作掩码攻击，在多种扑克游戏（6到5531个信息状态）和两个非扑克领域中进行实验，对比随机掩码和动作扰动基线，测试多种算法受害者（Q-learning、PPO、NFSP、neural NFSP、DQN）并验证跨智能体迁移性和自对弈放大效应。

Result: 学习型掩码攻击比随机掩码和扰动基线造成显著更大的伤害；攻击可跨算法迁移；自对弈放大攻击效果；即使在延长掩码训练下也无法恢复；提出了可达加权条件动作容量（CAC_w）和值加权CAC_v两种度量指标揭示攻击机制。

Conclusion: 动作可用性是自对弈强化学习中一个独特的鲁棒性攻击面，需要单独关注和防御。

Abstract: We study adversarial action masking in self-play reinforcement learning: an attacker selectively removes legal actions from a victim's action set. Unlike observation or action perturbations, removal eliminates decision options before the agent acts. Across poker games scaling from 6 to 5,531 information states and two non-poker domains, learned masking causes substantially more damage than random masking and learned perturbation baselines. The attack persists across Q-learning, PPO, NFSP, neural NFSP, and DQN victims; transfers across agents; is amplified by self-play; and shows no recovery under extended masked training. Mechanistically, the adversary targets high-value decision points, captured by reach-weighted contingent action capacity (CAC$_w$) and a value-weighted refinement CAC$_v$. These results identify action availability as a distinct robustness surface in self-play RL.

</details>


### [100] [A Structural Threshold in Decision Capacity Governs Collapse in Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16315)
*Arahan Kujur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，在自我对弈强化学习中，决策容量存在一个阈值：当所有正到达率的条件决策点被消除时，智能体将崩溃至确定性剥削吸引子（即接近最大损失的固定点）；只要保留一个正到达率的条件决策点，就能阻止这种崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自我对弈强化学习在非对称规则扰动下是否会崩溃，以及其背后的机制是协同适应还是扰动本身。

Method: 在多种扑克变体、矩阵博弈、骰子游戏中使用多种学习算法，通过消除正到达率条件决策点进行实验，并设置了冻结基线和固定对手对照组。

Result: 消除所有正到达率条件决策点导致快速收敛到确定性剥削吸引子（近最大损失固定点）；保留一个即可避免崩溃；该现象与时间无关，恢复动作可逆，且函数逼近会加剧该现象。

Conclusion: 在零到达加权条件动作容量处存在一个尖锐阈值，且在测试领域中，严重程度随到达加权容量的变化连续缩放。

Abstract: We show that a threshold in decision capacity determines whether self-play reinforcement learning agents collapse under asymmetric rule perturbations. Across poker variants, matrix games, a dice game, and multiple learning algorithms, eliminating all positive-reach contingent decisions causes rapid convergence to a deterministic exploitation attractor, a fixed point at near-maximal loss. Preserving even a single positive-reach contingent decision point prevents this collapse. A frozen baseline and fixed-opponent control confirm that the mechanism is co-adaptation under constraint, not the perturbation itself. The phenomenon is timing-invariant, fully reversible upon action restoration, and intensifies under function approximation. These results establish a sharp threshold at zero reach-weighted contingent action capacity, with severity scaling continuously via reach-weighted capacity in the tested domains.

</details>


### [101] [Forecasting Medium-Horizon Alzheimer's Disease Progression: Residual Gap-Aware Transformers for 24-Month CDR-SB Change from ADNI Clinical and Biomarker Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16319)
*Ran Tong,Tong Wang,Lanruo Wang,Yin Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于锚点的残差感知Transformer，用于阿尔茨海默病中程进展预测，结合混合效应统计参考和Transformer残差学习，有效处理不规则生物标志物历史数据，在CDR-SB预测上比传统方法MSE降低13.1%，相关性提升26.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中程阿尔茨海默病进展预测困难，原因在于未来临床评分与基线严重程度紧密相关，而生物标志物历史数据不规则且观测不完整，现有方法难以有效处理这些问题。

Method: 提出残差感知Transformer：1) 以轻度认知障碍访视为锚点构建样本；2) 混合效应模型作为统计参考（含受试者级随机截距）；3) Transformer进行残差学习，采用三元组标记化处理不规则历史，并在自注意力中引入学习到的非负时间间隔惩罚。

Result: 在2,600个锚点样本（858名参与者）上，相比线性混合效应基线，MSE降低13.1%，预测-观测相关性提升26.4%；优于GRU-D和STraTS；在五次受试者级随机划分中均取得最佳平均测试性能。

Conclusion: 统计锚定和间隔感知残差学习为中程阿尔茨海默病进展预测提供了有效的结构化框架。

Abstract: Medium-horizon Alzheimer's disease progression prediction is difficult because future clinical scores can remain tied to baseline severity, while biomarker histories are irregular and incompletely observed. We develop an anchor-based analysis of 24-month Clinical Dementia Rating Sum of Boxes (CDR-SB) change using harmonized Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) tables. Each labeled sample is anchored at a mild cognitive impairment visit, uses only clinical and biomarker history observed at or before that anchor, and defines the response as CDR-SB at the future visit closest to 24 months within an 18--30 month window minus anchor CDR-SB. The analytic cohort contains 2,600 labeled anchors from 858 participants and 7,276 longitudinal rows. We propose a residual gap-aware transformer that combines a mixed-effects statistical reference with transformer-based residual learning from pre-anchor clinical and biomarker histories. The model uses participant-level random intercepts in the mixed-effects reference, observation-level triplet tokenization for irregular histories, and a learned nonnegative time-gap penalty inside self-attention. We compare the proposed model with a Bayesian-information-criterion-selected linear mixed-effects baseline, GRU-D, and STraTS under repeated participant-level train--test splits. Across five participant-level random seeds, the proposed model achieves the best mean test performance across all reported metrics, reducing MSE by 13.1% and increasing prediction--observation correlation by 26.4% relative to the mixed-effects baseline. It also improves over both GRU-D and STraTS in mean error and correlation. These results show that statistical anchoring and gap-aware residual learning provide a useful structure for medium-horizon Alzheimer's disease progression prediction.

</details>


### [102] [AdaGraph: A Graph-Native Clustering Algorithm That Overcomes the Curse of Dimensionality and Enables Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16320)
*Ahmed Elmahdi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaGraph是一种基于图拓扑的无监督聚类算法，通过结构中心计算替代距离中心计算来克服维度灾难。在10个合成基准（d=10到5000）和三个科学领域（基因表达、文本聚类、材料科学）中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于距离的聚类算法在高维空间中面临维度灾难问题，欧氏距离等度量变得无信息。论文旨在通过结构中心（拓扑）计算范式取代几何中心（距离）计算，从根本上解决高维聚类的难题。

Method: 提出AdaGraph算法，完全在kNN图拓扑内操作；引入Graph-SCOPE作为基于拓扑的聚类有效性指标用于无监督调优；采用SLCD（Sample-Learn-Calibrate-Deploy）原型-部署框架实现可扩展性；无需预先指定聚类数k，原生处理噪声。

Result: 合成基准上Graph-SCOPE均值ARI=0.900，9/10数据集正确选择k，Kendall tau≥0.92（Silhouette仅~0.46）。基因模块发现优于WGCNA等；文本聚类ARI=0.751（HDBSCAN仅0.464，提升62%）；三个材料科学数据集均取得最高Graph-SCOPE。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑的图原生聚类范式可有效克服维度灾难，在高维及跨领域任务中显著优于传统距离驱动的聚类方法，具有广泛的适用性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present AdaGraph, a graph-native clustering algorithm born from the Structure-Centric Machine Learning (SC-ML) paradigm -- a new field of unsupervised learning that replaces geometry-centric (distance-based) computation with structure-centric (topology-based) computation, fundamentally dissolving the curse of dimensionality. AdaGraph operates entirely within the kNN graph topology, a representation that retains meaningful relational structure in arbitrarily high dimensions where Euclidean distance metrics become uninformative. AdaGraph requires no a priori specification of the number of clusters k, handles noise natively, and scales via the SLCD (Sample-Learn-Calibrate-Deploy) prototype-deployment framework. As its unsupervised tuning objective, AdaGraph pairs with Graph-SCOPE, the topology-based cluster validity index introduced as a separate SC-ML contribution. On 10 synthetic benchmarks spanning d=10 to d=5000, Graph-SCOPE achieves mean ARI=0.900 and correctly selects k on 9/10 datasets -- outperforming Silhouette, Davies-Bouldin, and Calinski-Harabasz -- while maintaining Kendall tau >= 0.92 with ground-truth cluster quality across all dimensionalities (Silhouette: tau ~= 0.46). We validate AdaGraph across three scientific domains: (1) gene co-expression discovery in hepatocellular carcinoma (GSE14520, 10,000 genes, 488 patients, no dimensionality reduction), where AdaGraph identifies condition-specific gene modules that WGCNA, ICA, NMF, and Spectral Biclustering fail to resolve; (2) natural language text clustering, where AdaGraph achieves ARI=0.751 on 20NG-6cat versus HDBSCAN's 0.464 (62% relative improvement); (3) materials science clustering of superconductors (145-dimensional Magpie features), perovskites, and JARVIS-DFT materials, where AdaGraph achieves the highest Graph-SCOPE on all three datasets.

</details>


### [103] [Orth-Dion: Eliminating Geometric Mismatch in Distributed Low-Rank Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16341)
*Tatsuhiro Nakamori,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Ganesh Talluri,Ansh Tiwari,Hideyuki Kawashima,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Guillaume Rabusseau,Hiroki Naganuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Orth-Dion方法，通过QR正交化替代列归一化，修复了Dion方法中低秩梯度压缩与谱优化器之间的几何不匹配问题，在相同通信成本下达到了与全秩谱方法相同的收敛速率O(√(L_r/T))，并通过大语言模型预训练实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dion方法虽兼容全分片数据并行训练，但收敛速度慢于全秩谱方法。作者发现该差距源于几何原因：列归一化无法得到Muon隐式目标的秩-r极分解因子，导致方向违反低秩谱几何的对偶范数约束，收敛速率额外增加√r因子。

Method: 提出Orth-Dion，将Dion中的列归一化替换为QR正交化；采用自洽不动点论证去除误差反馈分析中常用的有界漂移假设；使用时间平均收缩技术，仅需误差序列在平均意义上收缩而非每一步收缩。

Result: Orth-Dion在非欧几里得光滑性条件下达到O(√(L_r/T))的收敛速率，与全秩谱方法匹配；大语言模型预训练实验验证了√r缩放规律，表明Orth-Dion以Dion的通信成本弥合了与Muon的收敛差距。

Conclusion: 通过修复几何不匹配，Orth-Dion在保持Dion通信效率的同时实现了与全秩谱优化器相当的收敛性能，理论分析和实验均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank gradient compression reduces communication in distributed training by representing updates with rank-$r$ factors. Dion is a recent method that approximates Muon, a spectral optimizer that orthogonalizes momentum, using one step of power iteration followed by column normalization (rescaling each column of the right factor to unit length). This makes it compatible with fully sharded data parallel training, but it converges more slowly than full-rank spectral methods. We show that this gap is geometric: column normalization does not yield the rank-$r$ polar factor that Muon implicitly targets, so the resulting direction violates the dual-norm constraint of the low-rank spectral geometry, and the rate picks up an extra factor of $\sqrt{r}$ even though the low-rank approximation of the gradient itself is accurate. The same mismatch enters the smoothness term and the error-feedback recursion in the analysis, which has a knock-on effect on empirical performance. We propose Orth-Dion, which replaces column normalization with QR orthogonalization of the right factor. Under non-Euclidean smoothness, with $L_r$ the curvature constant along rank-$r$ directions, Orth-Dion attains rate $O(\sqrt{L_r/T})$, matching exact spectral methods at the same per-step communication cost as Dion. The proof removes the bounded-drift assumption common in prior error-feedback analyses via a self-consistent fixed-point argument, and uses a time-averaged contraction that only requires the error sequence to contract on average rather than at every step. Experiments on large-scale language model pre-training validate the predicted $\sqrt{r}$ scaling and show that Orth-Dion closes the convergence gap to Muon at Dion's communication cost.

</details>


### [104] [LoopQ: Quantization for Recursive Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16343)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究循环语言模型(LoopLM)的量化问题，提出LoopQ框架，通过循环感知的量化方法在4比特量化下提升平均下游准确率68.8%，降低困惑度87.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过递归复用Transformer块提升参数效率，但参数复用导致其在训练后量化(PTQ)下更加脆弱，目前缺乏对LoopLM量化的系统研究。

Method: 提出LoopQ框架，包含激活值缩放、选择性变换、跨循环状态对齐和轨迹感知优化四个组件，在保持共享量化主干的同时引入轻量级适配。

Result: 在七个基准测试上，W4A4量化下相比最强静态PTQ基线，下游准确率平均提升68.8%，困惑度平均降低87.7%。

Conclusion: 循环感知的量化方法能有效解决LoopLM中跨角色分布偏移、循环间状态复用和递归误差累积三个关键挑战。

Abstract: Looped language models (LoopLMs) improve parameter efficiency by recursively reusing Transformer blocks, enabling deeper computation under a fixed model size. However, this reuse makes LoopLMs more fragile under post-training quantization (PTQ). We present the first systematic study of quantization in LoopLMs and identify three challenges: distribution shift across roles, state reuse across loop transitions, and recursive error accumulation. To address these challenges, we propose LoopQ, a loop-aware PTQ framework that preserves a shared quantized backbone while introducing lightweight adaptations. LoopQ combines activation scaling, selective transformation, cross-loop state alignment, and trajectory-aware optimization to reduce distributional mismatch within loops and error accumulation across loops. Experiments across seven benchmarks show that, under W4A4 quantization, LoopQ improves average downstream accuracy by 68.8% and reduces average perplexity by 87.7% compared with the strongest static PTQ baseline.

</details>


### [105] [Goal-Conditioned Supervised Learning for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16345)
*Shijun Li,Kaiwen Dong,Xiang Gao,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为目标条件监督学习（GCSL）的离线微调框架，将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，通过纯监督学习让LLM生成达到该目标的回复，并引入新的目标公式（追求超出质量阈值的结果方向性提升），在三个任务上优于传统离线微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法中，在线方法（如RL-based）成本高、部署难；离线方法（如SFT、DPO）存在局限性：SFT将分级反馈压缩为二元监督，DPO依赖难以获取的配对偏好数据。需要一种既高效又能充分利用分级反馈的离线对齐方法。

Method: 提出GCSL框架：1) 将反馈信号直接作为显式目标，通过监督学习训练模型生成达到该目标的回复；2) 引入新目标公式——学习持续追求超出目标质量阈值的结果，而非模仿高质量子集样本，以缓解有界学习效应；3) 采用自然语言目标表示以利用LLM的语义理解和推理能力。

Result: 在无害生成、代码生成和推荐系统三个任务上，该方法在保持监督学习效率、可扩展性和简单数据要求的同时，一致优于标准离线微调基线。

Conclusion: GCSL作为一种纯监督学习的离线对齐框架，既能避免在线方法的高成本，又能克服SFT和DPO的局限性，有效利用分级反馈信号，是一种高效且实用的LLM对齐方案。

Abstract: Large language models often require fine-tuning to better align their behavior with user intent at deployment. Existing approaches are commonly divided into online and offline paradigms. Online methods, such as RL-based alignment, can directly optimize outcome quality but typically rely on external reward models and iterative rollouts, making them costly and difficult to deploy in many cases. Offline methods are more efficient, but prevailing approaches such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and direct preference optimization (DPO) remain limited: SFT typically collapses graded feedback into binary supervision, while DPO depends on paired preference data that is often unavailable or expensive to construct.
  In this paper, we propose goal-conditioned supervised learning (GCSL) as an offline fine-tuning framework for LLMs. Our core idea is to treat feedback signals directly as an explicit goal and train the model, purely through supervised learning, to generate responses that achieve that goal. To better exploit graded feedback, we further introduce a novel goal formulation that defines learning as consistently pursuing outcomes above a target quality threshold, rather than imitating samples from a selected high-quality subset. This design mitigates the bounded-learning effect of SFT and classic GCSL by explicitly guiding the model to learn the directional progression of quality. We also propose natural-language goal representations to better leverage the semantic understanding and reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We evaluate our method on three tasks: non-toxic generation, code generation, and LLM for recommendation. Results show that our approach consistently outperforms standard offline fine-tuning baselines while retaining the efficiency, scalability, and simple data requirements of supervised learning.

</details>


### [106] [PropGuard: Safeguarding LLM-MAS via Propagation-Aware Exploration and Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16346)
*Bingyu Yan,Xiaoming Zhang,Jinyu Hou,Chaozhuo Li,Ziyi Zhou,Xiaozhe Zhang,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PropGuard框架，通过双视角时空图构建、GE-GRPO训练检测器和子图感知修复，防御LLM多智能体系统中恶意指令的传播攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中，恶意指令可通过消息、工具、记忆在智能体间跨轮次传播，现有防御方法无法追踪细粒度传播路径或修复污染状态而不破坏良性协作。

Method: 构建响应中心风险估计与全状态证据保存的双视角时空图；使用GE-GRPO训练的检测器顺序探索全状态图，恢复紧凑可疑传播子图；通过子图感知诊断验证有害传播，并应用源引导修复纠正上游污染、重放受影响下游交互。

Result: 在四种通信架构和五种攻击设置下，PropGuard持续降低攻击成功率，同时保持高任务级防御成功率，实现有效性-效率的良好权衡。

Conclusion: PropGuard是一种传播感知的安全框架，能有效追踪和修复LLM多智能体系统中的恶意指令传播，且不破坏良性协作。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) have become a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks through role specialization, tool use, memory, and collaborative reasoning. However, these interactions create new security risks that malicious instructions injected through messages, tools, or memories can propagate across agents and rounds, causing system-level compromise. Existing defenses largely rely on local filtering or graph-based anomaly detection, but they often fail to trace fine-grained propagation paths or remediate contaminated states without disrupting benign collaboration. We propose PropGuard, a propagation-aware framework for safeguarding LLM-MAS. PropGuard constructs a dual-view spatio-temporal graph that combines response-centric risk estimation with full-state evidence preservation. Guided by these risk priors, a GE-GRPO trained inspector sequentially explores the full-state graph to recover compact suspicious propagation subgraphs. PropGuard then verifies harmful propagation through subgraph-aware diagnosis and applies source-guided remediation to correct upstream contamination and replay affected downstream interactions. Experiments across four communication architectures and five attack settings demonstrate that PropGuard consistently lowers attack success while maintaining high task-level defense success, achieving a favorable effectiveness--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [107] [HPC-LLM: Practical Domain Adaptation and Retrieval-Augmented Generation for HPC Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16347)
*Nourin Shahin,Izzat Alsmadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HPC-LLM，一个面向高性能计算场景的检索增强与领域适配助手，通过集成文档检索、QLoRA微调与本地推理，在保持较低资源开销的同时，使8B模型性能接近14B通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研人员在使用HPC基础设施（如Slurm调度器、GPU资源、MPI并行框架）时面临较高操作门槛，现有通用LLM缺乏HPC领域的专业运维知识，无法提供可靠支持。

Method: 构建HPC领域语料库（含大学文档、运维示例、合成问答对，约9K-24K样本）；基于Llama 3.1 8B使用QLoRA进行轻量级领域微调；集成自动化文档摄入、密集检索与模块化编排管道；在JetStream2平台上进行评估。

Result: 领域适配后的8B模型在检索增强设置下，性能接近Qwen 2.5 14B等更大规模通用模型，同时GPU显存需求与推理延迟显著更低。

Conclusion: 通过检索增强与轻量级领域适配，较小规模的LLM可以在HPC场景中高效工作，降低资源消耗的同时保持良好性能。

Abstract: Modern scientific research increasingly depends on High-Performance Computing (HPC) infrastructures, yet many researchers face significant operational barriers when interacting with cluster environments, job schedulers, GPU resources, and parallel computing frameworks. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) provide useful coding assistance but often lack the domain-specific operational knowledge required for reliable HPC support. This paper presents HPC-LLM, a retrieval augmented and domain-adapted assistant designed to support common HPC workflows including Slurm scheduling, MPI execution, GPU utilization, filesystem management, and cluster troubleshooting. The proposed framework integrates automated documentation ingestion, dense retrieval, lightweight domain adaptation using QLoRA, and local inference within a modular orchestration pipeline. To support domain adaptation, we construct an HPC-oriented corpus from publicly available university HPC documentation, curated operational examples, and synthetic instruction-answer pairs generated from retrieved HPC content. The resulting dataset contains approximately 9,000 to 24,000 HPC-focused training examples spanning job scheduling, GPU computing, distributed training, storage systems, and cluster administration topics. We fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B using QLoRA and evaluate the resulting model against several open weight baselines under retrieval-augmented settings on JetStream2 infrastructure. Experimental results indicate that the adapted 8B model achieves performance comparable to substantially larger general-purpose models while operating under significantly lower GPU memory requirements and inference latency. In particular, the adapted model approaches the performance of Qwen 2.5 14B while requiring substantially fewer computational resources.

</details>


### [108] [Flow-Direct: Feedback-Efficient and Reusable Guidance for Flow Models via Non-Parametric Guidance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16348)
*Kim Yong Tan,Yueming Lyu,Ivor Tsang,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flow-Direct提出一种基于持久化引导场的无训练扩散/流模型引导框架，通过非参数密度比估计器累积所有奖励样本，实现高效反馈利用和可复用多目标生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练引导方法反馈效率低，因为奖励反馈仅用于局部的梯度近似或离散搜索决策，随后便被丢弃，造成信息浪费。

Method: 从奖励加权目标分布与基础分布的对数密度比出发，解析推导出持久化引导场；实践中通过非参数估计器累积所有奖励评估样本构建该引导场，引导场随样本增多而逐渐精确。

Result: Flow-Direct具有两个优势：1）反馈高效，每个评估样本都用于优化全局引导场，不浪费任何奖励信息；2）自然可复用，优化完成后数据集定义了可复用的引导场，无需额外奖励评估即可生成新样本，不同引导场可组合实现多目标生成。

Conclusion: Flow-Direct通过持久化引导场解决了现有方法反馈效率低的问题，并实现了引导场的可复用性和多目标组合能力。

Abstract: Training-free guidance enables pre-trained diffusion and flow models to optimize application-specific objectives using feedback from external black-box reward functions. However, existing methods are feedback-inefficient because reward feedback is used only transiently to inform a localized gradient approximation or a discrete search decision, and is subsequently discarded. To address this limitation, we propose Flow-Direct, a framework that guides the generation process via a persistent guidance field. Theoretically, this guidance field is analytically derived from the log-density ratio between the base and reward-weighted target distributions; it transports the pre-trained distribution to the target distribution. In practice, the field is implemented as a non-parametric estimator constructed from all accumulated reward-evaluated samples. As more samples are collected during optimization, this empirical guidance field becomes increasingly accurate. This persistent formulation yields two major advantages. First, Flow-Direct is highly feedback-efficient: because every evaluated sample is used to refine the global guidance field, no reward information is wasted. Second, the framework is naturally reusable: once optimization is complete, the collected dataset defines a reusable guidance field for generating novel target samples without additional reward evaluations, and distinct guidance fields can be combined to generate samples that simultaneously satisfy multiple objectives.

</details>


### [109] [Geometric Asymmetry in MoE Specialization: Functional Decorrelation and Representational Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16349)
*Feilong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann 统一框架分析 MoE 层，发现专家在功能上高度去相关但在表示子空间上存在重叠，路由稀疏性是影响这种几何结构的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 架构通过稀疏路由实现可扩展容量，但专家专业化的几何结构仍不清晰，缺乏统一的定量分析框架。

Method: 提出 Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann 框架，在函数空间和表示空间两个层面分析 MoE 层，并在 Mistral、Qwen 等预训练 MoE Transformer 上进行实验，同时设计了可控路由实验。

Result: 专家间 Jacobian 对齐度极低（功能去相关），但表示子空间存在部分重叠；top-k 路由导致更尖锐的功能分离和更大的子空间差异，而 soft 路由使专家结构更纠缠。

Conclusion: MoE 层可被视为在共享表示流形的重叠子流形上实现局部去相关算子，路由稀疏性是塑造这种几何结构的关键因素。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures achieve scalable capacity through sparse routing, yet the geometric structure of expert specialization remains poorly understood. We introduce a unified Jacobian-PCA-Grassmann framework for analyzing MoE layers in both function space and representation space. Across pretrained MoE Transformers (Mistral, Qwen), we find a consistent structural asymmetry: experts exhibit strong functional decorrelation (consistently low, near-zero cross-expert Jacobian alignment) while their routed representations occupy distinct but partially overlapping subspaces. This indicates that functional decorrelation and representation overlap coexist rather than coincide in MoE specialization. Controlled routing experiments further indicate that routing sparsity appears to be a key factor shaping this geometry: top-k routing induces sharper functional separation and larger subspace divergence, whereas fully soft routing yields more entangled expert structure. Together, these results suggest a geometric interpretation in which MoE layers may be viewed as implementing locally decorrelated operators over overlapping submanifolds on a shared representation manifold, and provide a general diagnostic framework for studying conditional computation in modern Transformer architectures.

</details>


### [110] [Federated Nested Learning: Collaborative Training of Self-Referential Memories for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16350)
*Hong Chen,Pengcheng Wu,Yuanguo Lin,Peilin Zhao,Xiuze Zhou,Fan Lin,Han Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦嵌套学习（FedNL），将联邦学习重构为三级嵌套优化系统，利用Titans线性注意力机制实现轻量级零样本测试时适应，在非独立同分布数据上提升长上下文检索和流式交叉熵性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习仅协作学习静态模型，难以处理客户端非独立同分布（Non-IID）数据。作者从嵌套学习视角重新审视FL，认为核心挑战在于如何协作学习优化规则而非仅静态模型。

Method: 提出FedNL框架，将FL重构为三级嵌套优化系统。嵌入基于Titans的线性注意力机制，通过将delta规则作为在线梯度步骤，使客户端能够进行轻量级、零样本的测试时自适应。

Result: 在Non-IID MMLU和长上下文基准测试上，FedNL在短上下文推理上具有竞争力，提升了长上下文检索和流式交叉熵性能，并保持恒定的推理内存。

Conclusion: FedNL通过嵌套优化和线性注意力机制有效解决了FL中Non-IID数据挑战，在保持推理效率的同时提升了模型在长上下文任务上的表现。

Abstract: We rethink Federated Learning (FL) from a nested learning perspective, framing the core challenge as how to collaboratively learn optimization rules, not just static models, to tackle Non-IID client data. To address this, we propose Federated Nested Learning (FedNL), a novel framework that reformulates FL as a three-level nested optimization system. FedNL embeds Titans-based linear attention into FL, enabling clients to perform lightweight, zero-shot test-time adaptation by treating a delta rule as an online gradient step. Experiments on Non-IID MMLU and long-context benchmarks show that FedNL achieves competitive performance in short-context reasoning, enhances the performance of long-context retrieval and streaming Cross-Entropy, and maintains constant inference memory.

</details>


### [111] [Augmenting Human Evaluation with LLM Judges: How Many Human Reviews Do You Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16354)
*Jane Paik Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM评估者从替代人类评价的角色转变为辅助角色，采用两阶段抽样设计，并引入双重稳健估计量来提高评估效率，同时指导人机评价样本量的分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM作为评估者的方法缺乏形式化研究设计基础，仅靠人机一致性指标来替代人类评价，缺乏对样本量和人类监督程度的系统性指导。

Method: (1) 将LLM评价者角色从替代性转为辅助性；(2) 提出两阶段抽样设计：第一阶段用LLM评价所有样本，第二阶段对子样本进行人工评价；(3) 采用缺失数据文献中的双重稳健估计量；(4) 利用渐近方差指导人机样本量分配。

Result: 证明了双重稳健估计量在设计已知的缺失模型下具有鲁棒性；提出了如何确定人机评价样本量以达到目标统计功效；发现当LLM评价可预测性不高时，应分配更多人工评价。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM作为评估者提供了形式化的统计框架，填补了在验证基准时人类监督程度缺乏指导的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as automated evaluators of AI systems, including in high-stakes applications. In this role, LLMs are used to generate judgments about the quality, appropriateness, or even safety of model outputs. This approach is motivated by practical constraints. Expert human ratings are costly and difficult to scale, whereas LLM ratings can be produced quickly at low cost. However, current approaches to deploying LLM evaluators are ad hoc, typically limited to reporting agreement metrics between human and LLM judges as a justification for substitution of human ratings, and lack a formal basis for study design. This paper (1) shifts the role of the LLM judge from substitutive to auxiliary, and (2) formulates the LLM-as-a-judge paradigm as one of augmenting human evaluation through a two-stage sampling design, where LLM evaluations are measured for all observations at the first stage and human ratings are partially observed for a subsample at the second stage. We propose to use a doubly robust estimator from the missing data literature, which takes advantage of the robustness property against the prediction model, since the missingness model is known by design. Using the asymptotic variance of this estimator, we propose how sample sizes of human and LLM ratings can be determined to achieve a targeted level of power. We also show that a study can be efficiently designed by allocating more human ratings for types of evaluations where the predictability of LLM ratings is not high. To the best of our knowledge, there is very little guidance on how much human oversight should be retained when validating benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [LEAF: A Living Benchmark for Event-Augmented Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16358)
*Mingtian Tan,Mihir Parmar,Palash Goyal,Chun-Liang Li,Nanyun Peng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LEAF是首个面向事件增强预测任务的动态基准测试，通过递归检索智能体系统和双智能体交叉验证，评估大语言模型在复杂真实场景下的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测基准存在两个问题：要么因数据稀缺而缺乏多维事件信息（这对准确预测至关重要），要么局限于相对封闭的环境。因此需要构建一个能评估LLM在复杂真实场景下预测能力的动态基准。

Method: 提出LEAF基准，包含未来事件概率预测、趋势预测和时间序列预测三大任务；采用递归检索智能体系统（recursive retrieval agent system）配合双智能体交叉验证（dual-agent cross-validation），为预测提供全面且相关的辅助文本信息。

Result: 评估了最先进的专有和开源LLM，发现模型能够利用从复杂事件中提取的信号提升预测性能；在股票领域，LLM在其确信可预测性更高的股票上表现更好；事件与目标股票之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: LEAF为事件驱动预测任务提供了一个必要的、动态更新的测试平台，能够持续追踪和推动该领域的进展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to forecasting. To evaluate this capability while mitigating pre-training data contamination, several living benchmarks have been proposed. However, existing benchmarks either lack the multidimensional events essential for accurate forecasting due to data scarcity, or focus on relatively closed environments. To assess the predictive capabilities of LLMs in complex, real-world scenarios, we propose LEAF, the first living benchmark for event-augmented forecasting tasks, including future event probabilities, trend and time series forecasting. LEAF utilizes a recursive retrieval agent system paired with dual-agent cross-validation to provide comprehensive and relevant auxiliary text for forecasting. Evaluating state-of-the-art proprietary and open-weight LLMs, we find that these models can leverage signals extracted from complex events to enhance predictive performance. In the stock domain, we find that LLMs achieve better performance on equities they confidently identify as more predictable. Furthermore, the events demonstrate a strong correlation with the target equities. To this end, LEAF provides a necessary, dynamically updating testbed to continuously track and drive progress in event-driven forecasting tasks.

</details>


### [113] [TailedTS: Benchmark Dataset for Heavy-Tailed Time Series Prediction and Periodicity Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16361)
*Xinyu Chen,HanQin Cai,Lijun Ding,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TailedTS是一个基于2024年维基百科每小时页面浏览量构建的大规模时间序列基准数据集，包含约246.9亿数据点，用于测试模型在重尾、零膨胀和非高斯分布下的鲁棒性。高流量页面呈现幂律分布（5%页面贡献70%流量），且周期性更弱。实验表明高斯损失函数在高流量类别上表现退化，而鲁棒替代方法一致更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基准（如M4、M5、UCI电力数据集）缺乏重尾、零膨胀、非高斯分布的真实场景，无法充分检验模型在面对极端波动时的鲁棒性。作者希望通过维基百科流量数据构建一个自然呈现幂律分布的大规模基准，填补这一空白。

Method: 从2024年全年维基百科每小时页面浏览数据中提取约246.9亿数据点，覆盖约300万独立页面/月，以Apache Parquet高效格式存储。提出基于稀疏自回归（带稀疏性与非负性约束）的周期性量化框架，并在多种非高斯损失函数（ℓ1、Huber、分位数、ℓp范数）下建立标准化预测基准。

Result: 高频页面（top 5%）贡献超70%总流量，呈现显著幂律分布；高频页面周期性显著弱于低频页面；标准高斯损失函数在高流量页面类别上性能严重退化，而鲁棒损失函数在所有流量规模上保持一致增益。

Conclusion: TailedTS为重尾、零膨胀时间序列预测提供了有挑战性的标准化基准，揭示了大流量场景下高斯假设的局限性，强调了鲁棒损失函数的必要性，对大型数字平台的流量预测与服务器资源分配具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: We present TailedTS, a large-scale benchmark dataset derived from Wikipedia hourly page view observations throughout 2024, specifically designed to test time series forecasting models under heavy-tailed, zero-inflated, and non-Gaussian conditions. The dataset comprises approximately 24.69 billion data points spanning roughly 3 million unique Wikipedia pages per month, stored in high-efficiency Apache Parquet format. Wikipedia traffic follows a pronounced power-law distribution where roughly 5% of pages account for over 70% of total page views, creating a natural and rigorous testbed for model robustness against extreme volatility that are absent from or underrepresented in existing benchmarks such as M4, M5, and UCI electricity datasets. TailedTS enables several research tasks. First, we introduce a periodicity quantification framework based on sparse autoregression with sparsity and non-negativity constraints, revealing that frequently-viewed pages exhibit significantly weaker periodic structure than their less-viewed counterparts, showing direct implications for server allocation and traffic forecasting on large digital platforms. Second, we provide standardized prediction benchmarks evaluated under a suite of non-Gaussian loss functions, including $\ell_1$-norm, Huber, quantile, and $\ell_p$-norm losses, demonstrating that standard Gaussian-based estimators degrade substantially on high-volume page categories, while robust alternatives provide consistent gains across all traffic scales. TailedTS is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.17070469.

</details>


### [114] [When Is Rank-1 Steering Cheap? Geometry, Granularity, and Budgeted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16362)
*John T. Robertson,Jianing Zhu,Haris Vikalo,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRACE框架，通过激活几何结构指导的搜索方法，有效提升秩一激活引导的效率，并引入“概念粒度”指标解释不同概念下引导效果的差异。研究将问题从“秩一何时失效”转变为“秩一何时廉价且稳定”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作发现激活引导在不同概念上的效果差异很大，常归因于许多概念无法被单一方向捕捉。本文认为这种变异性主要源于搜索困难——有用的秩一干预往往存在，但找到它成本高昂。

Method: 形式化秩一引导为预算受限的优化问题（干预层和系数）；提出基于提示边界方向对齐的几何引导搜索；引入“概念粒度”度量不同对比上下文间的方向异质性；构建GRACE框架，利用激活几何诊断困难来源、选择补救措施并高效分配优化资源。

Result: 几何引导搜索在三个模型家族中平均减少39.8%的尝试次数即可达到最佳效用的95%；概念粒度与收敛速度（r=0.44）和最佳效用（r=-0.46）显著相关（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 本文转变研究视角，从“何时秩一无效”转向“何时秩一廉价且稳定”，将激活几何从描述性工具转化为可操作的先验知识，用于高效控制大语言模型。

Abstract: Activation steering offers a lightweight way to control LLMs without retraining, but its effectiveness varies sharply across concepts. Prior work often reads this variability as evidence that many concepts are not captured by a single steering direction. We argue instead that much of it reflects search difficulty: a useful rank-1 intervention often exists, but finding it can be expensive.
  We formalize rank-1 steering as a budget-constrained optimization over intervention layer and coefficient. Across concepts and model families, prompt-boundary directional alignment predicts where effective interventions occur, enabling geometry-guided search that reaches high utility with substantially fewer evaluations, reducing the trials needed to recover 95\% of best-found utility by 39.8\% on average across three model families. To explain why some concepts remain expensive even under better search, we introduce \emph{concept granularity}, a measure of directional heterogeneity across contrastive contexts. Granularity distinguishes concepts whose difference vectors share a stable global direction from those where prompts agree locally within each input but the utility-maximizing direction rotates systematically across inputs.
