Within each modality, MedMIX aggregates complementary embeddings from multiple small expert models; across modalities, it performs learned fusion over available modalities; and during training, it leverages large teacher models to improve deployed representations without additional inference cost. Across three heterogeneous benchmarks (OpenI, MIMIC-IV-MM, and MMIST-ccRCC), MedMIX achieves consistently strong performance while remaining robust under controlled missing-modality perturbations, and further demonstrates sustained robustness under cross-cohort shift on MIMIC-III. These results highlight MedMIX as a practical framework that unifies within-modality expert collaboration, sample-specific cross-modality fusion, and efficient large--small model collaboration while remaining robust to incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [152] [Provably Shorter Scratchpads in Hybrid DeltaNet-Attention Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16640)
*Tomasz Steifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Qwen3-Next等开源语言模型中使用的混合循环注意力解码器（Gated Attention + Gated DeltaNet），证明了这种混合架构在表达能力上的形式化优势：在奇偶条件检索任务中，混合架构只需恒定长度的CoT即可解决，而纯Gated DeltaNet无法解决，纯Gated Attention则需要多项式长度的CoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究混合循环注意力解码器（Gated Attention + Gated DeltaNet）相比纯Gated DeltaNet或纯Gated Attention是否存在形式化的表达能力或效率优势。

Method: 定义了"奇偶条件检索任务"（parity-conditioned retrieval task），在恒定精度假设下，分别分析混合架构、纯Gated DeltaNet、纯Gated Attention在该任务上的表现，比较它们所需的Chain-of-Thought步骤长度。

Result: 混合架构（Qwen风格）只需O(1)的CoT步骤即可解决奇偶条件检索任务；纯Gated DeltaNet无法解决该任务；纯Gated Attention需要至少多项式长度的CoT步骤。

Conclusion: 混合架构在特定检索任务上具有形式化的表达能力优势，这种优势体现在显著减少所需的推理计算量（CoT步骤）上。

Abstract: We investigate the expressive power of hybrid recurrent-attention decoders, a class of architectures used in recent open-source language models such as Qwen3-Next and its successors. These models combine Gated Attention heads with recurrent Gated DeltaNet heads. Is there a formal advantage, in terms of model expressivity or efficiency, to such a hybrid architecture? We show that there is. We define parity-conditioned retrieval task and show that under constant-precision assumption, a Qwen-style hybrid of Gated DeltaNet and Gated Attention solves this task with a constant scratchpad, or equivalently $O(1)$ chain-of-thought steps. In contrast, no similar solution exists for pure Gated DeltaNet models, while pure Gated Attention requires at least a polynomial scratchpad.

</details>


### [153] [In-context learning enables continental-scale subsurface temperature prediction from sparse local observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16665)
*Daniel O'Malley,Christopher W. Johnson,Javier E. Santos,Pablo Lara,Sandro Malusà,Bharat Srikishan,John Kath,Arnab Mazumder,Mohamed Mehana,David Coblentz,Nathan DeBardeleben,Earl Lawrence,Hari Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了In-Context Earth模型，一种基于Transformer的上下文学习方法，利用稀疏钻孔观测数据预测大陆尺度地下温度场，在美国本土达到4.7°C平均绝对误差，无需微调即可迁移至其他地质区域，并学习到未直接观测的地下属性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大陆尺度地下温度信息对地热资源评估和浅层地壳热传输研究至关重要，但钻孔测量成本高且稀疏，传统插值方法会平滑尖锐热异常，物理模型难以捕捉复杂热场特征。

Method: 基于Transformer的上下文学习（In-Context Learning）模型，将稀疏局部钻孔观测作为地质上下文，预测连续的温度-深度场，并输出校准的不确定性估计。

Result: 在美国本土MAE为4.7°C，优于物理模型、AlphaEarth嵌入模型、Transparent Earth模型和克里金法；K-S统计量2.5%表明不确定性校准良好；零样本迁移至加拿大阿尔伯塔（MAE 2.2°C）、澳大利亚（6.2°C）和英国（5.4°C）。

Conclusion: 上下文学习可利用稀疏钻孔观测实现大陆尺度地下表征，无需密集测量或区域特定重训练，模型学到了未直接观测的地下属性（地震波速、地球化学、地壳结构）的物理一致表征。

Abstract: Continental-scale knowledge of subsurface temperature is limited by the cost and sparsity of borehole measurements, but such information is essential for geothermal resource assessment and for understanding heat transport in the shallow crust. The thermal field reflects the interaction between lithology, crustal structure, radiogenic heat production, and advective fluid flow, sometimes producing sharp anomalies that are smoothed by conventional interpolation or difficult to capture with physical models. Here we introduce In-Context Earth, a transformer-based model that uses sparse local borehole observations as geological context to predict continuous temperature-at-depth fields with calibrated uncertainty. In the contiguous United States, the model achieves a mean absolute error of 4.7 °C, outperforming the physics-informed Stanford Thermal Model, a model based on AlphaEarth embeddings, the multimodal Transparent Earth model, and universal kriging, while resolving sharper thermal gradients in geothermal provinces. Its uncertainty estimates are well calibrated, with a Kolmogorov-Smirnov statistic of 2.5%. Without finetuning, the model adapts to Alberta, Australia, and the United Kingdom (UK) using only 20 local observations at inference time, maintaining high accuracy in geologically distinct test regions with a mean absolute error of 2.2 °C in Alberta, 6.2 °C in Australia, and 5.4 °C in the UK. Interpretability analyses show that the model learns internal representations of subsurface properties it never observes during training, including seismic velocities, geochemistry, and crustal structure, and uses these representations in physically consistent ways. More broadly, this work shows that in-context learning can use sparse borehole observations for continental-scale subsurface characterization, without requiring dense measurements or region-specific retraining.

</details>


### [154] [GraViti: Graph-Level Variational Autoencoders with Relaxed Permutation Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16668)
*Roman Bresson,Konstantinos Divriotis,Johannes F. Lutzeyer,Iakovos Evdaimon,Michalis Vazirgiannis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraViti是一种基于Transformer的图级变分自编码器，将整个图映射为紧凑的潜在向量，实现图级潜在空间，支持平滑插值和属性引导搜索，在分子基准上达到SOTA重构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多使用节点级嵌入，缺乏真正的图级潜在空间，限制了平滑插值、属性搜索等下游任务的能力。

Method: 基于Transformer的图级变分自编码器，将整个图编码为紧凑潜在向量，并采用单步解码；在具有规范节点顺序的领域（如分子）中，选择不强制置换不变性以实现一致重构。

Result: 在大数据集上达到SOTA重构精度，生成性能良好，解码样本遵循训练数据中的化学约束。

Conclusion: GraViti在保持实用样本质量的同时，提供了比复杂生成流水线更轻量的单步解码替代方案；并发现对于有规范节点顺序的领域，强制置换不变性可能不利于一致重构。

Abstract: We introduce GraViti, a transformer-based graph-level variational autoencoder that maps entire graphs to compact latent vectors. This design produces a true graph-level latent space that supports smooth interpolation, property-guided search, and other downstream tasks beyond the constraints of node-level embeddings. On molecular benchmarks, GraViti learns to decode valid samples that follow the chemical constraints present in the training data, showing that the model recovers domain rules directly from graph-level representations. We also show that, in domains where a reliable canonical node ordering exists such as molecules or bayesian networks, enforcing permutation invariance can prove detrimental for consistent reconstruction. GraViti achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy on large datasets, and provides solid generative performance. Its single-step decoding offers a lightweight alternative to more complex generation pipelines while maintaining practical sample quality.

</details>


### [155] [Identify Then Project: Contrastive Learning of Latent Dynamics from Partial Observations with Port-Hamiltonian Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16682)
*Peilun Li,Kaiyuan Tan,Daniel Moyer,Thomas Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段“识别-投影”框架，用于从部分高维观测中学习潜在端口-哈密顿系统，在保持动力学精度的同时强制执行物理一致性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分观测和高维观测下，直接对观测空间建模不可行，而潜在表示学习与物理感知建模相互耦合，现有方法难以兼顾识别精度与物理一致性。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段，对比学习教师模型从部分观测中学习连续时间潜在动力学；第二阶段，学生模型通过学习的仿射图表将教师表示和动力学投影到端口-哈密顿子流形上，实现物理一致的表征。同时对比了单阶段联合学习变体。

Result: 两阶段方法能保持教师模型的动力学精度同时强制执行物理结构，在耗散系统和高维视觉场景中比单阶段方法更可靠。

Conclusion: 仿射投影是对比学习潜在识别的仿射规范与端口-哈密顿系统之间的自然桥梁，两阶段教师-学生框架优于单阶段联合学习方案。

Abstract: Identifying latent state representations and dynamics is essential when direct modeling in observation space is infeasible, particularly under partial and high-dimensional observations. In such settings, representation learning and physics-aware modeling are inherently coupled. We study this problem for latent port-Hamiltonian systems, a structured class encompassing both conservative and dissipative dynamics. We propose a two-stage identify-then-project framework. First, a contrastive teacher learns continuous-time latent dynamics from partial observations. Then, a student projects the identified teacher representation and dynamics onto a port-Hamiltonian submanifold via a learned affine chart, yielding a physically consistent realization. As a conceptual counterfactual, we also consider a single-stage variant that jointly learns latent identification and port-Hamiltonian structure, but find it to be less reliable, motivating the proposed two-stage teacher-student framework. We show theoretically that affine projection is the natural bridge between the affine gauge of contrastive latent identification and the port-Hamiltonian systems. Empirically, we demonstrate that the proposed two-stage approach preserves the teacher's dynamics while enforcing physical structure, and performs more reliably than the single-stage alternative, particularly in dissipative regimes and high-dimensional visual settings.

</details>


### [156] [Scalable Knowledge Editing for Mixture-of-Experts LLMs via Tensor-Structured Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16686)
*Roman Maksimov,Vladimir Aletov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有知识编辑方法主要面向密集前馈层、而现代LLM越来越多采用MoE架构的现状，提出了一种类似MEMIT的MoE知识编辑框架，利用张量结构和Woodbury矩阵恒等式实现专家级别的精确更新，编辑质量媲美强基线且速度提升达6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法主要针对密集前馈层，但现代LLM越来越多采用MoE架构（具有更优的内存占用和推理效率），这导致大量生产模型缺乏原则性的编辑工具。

Method: 利用MoE层的张量结构在每个专家级别精确构建编辑目标，并应用Woodbury矩阵恒等式避免对全堆叠专家权重矩阵进行具体化或求逆，更新仅需固定低秩矩阵的求逆，无需额外的反向传播。

Result: 在主要KE指标上匹配强基线编辑质量，同时由于批处理MEMIT风格公式和Woodbury恒等式实现的低维求逆，编辑速度提升高达6倍。

Conclusion: 闭式参数修改型KE可以有效扩展到密集层之外，为现代稀疏LLM架构中的可扩展知识编辑开辟了一条路径。

Abstract: Knowledge editing (KE) provides a lightweight alternative to repeated fine-tuning of LLMs. However, most existing KE methods target dense feed-forward layers, while modern LLMs increasingly adopt Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures for their superior memory footprint and inference efficiency. This mismatch leaves a growing class of production models without principled editing tools. We propose a MEMIT-like framework for knowledge editing in MoE-based LLMs. Our method exploits the tensor structure of MoE layers to formulate the editing objective faithfully at the per expert level, and applies the Woodbury matrix identity to avoid materializing or inverting the full stacked matrix of expert weights. The resulting update reduces to inversions of fixed low-rank matrices and requires no additional backward passes. Empirically, our approach matches the editing quality of strong baselines on the main KE metrics while accelerating the editing procedure by up to 6x, owing to the batched MEMIT-style formulation and the low-dimensional inversions enabled by the Woodbury identity. These results show that closed-form, parameter-modifying KE can be extended efficiently beyond dense layers, opening a path toward scalable knowledge editing in modern sparse LLM architectures.

</details>


### [157] [Your SaaS Is an Insurance Product: A Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16699)
*Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了限额消费型SaaS产品（如LLM订阅、云平台）在数学结构上与保险产品同构，并提出基于精算科学的定价框架（频率-严重度分解、保费计算原理、蒙特卡洛准备金充足性分析），通过LLM和云平台两个领域的公开订阅层级进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前cs.LG/stat.ML领域缺乏处理限额消费型SaaS产品定价问题的精算工具和术语，而这类产品在结构上与保险产品高度相似（固定保费、随机需求、重置限额、组合级尾部风险暴露），作者希望填补这一方法论空白。

Method: 采用频率-严重度分解（frequency-severity decomposition）对用户需求建模，结合保费计算原则（premium calculation principles）和蒙特卡洛模拟评估准备金充足性（reserve adequacy），将框架映射到LLM服务和云平台两个领域的公开订阅层级上。

Result: 通过一个实例展示了传统单位经济学（unit economics）与所提精算框架在定价上的显著偏离，验证了该框架的实际效用和与保险经济学（Arrow 1963; Pauly 1968等）的理论关联。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是操作性的而非理论性的：为cs.LG/stat.ML实践提供了一套已被精算科学验证的词汇和分析工具，而非提出新的数学定理，为限额消费型SaaS产品的风险定价提供了严谨的方法论基础。

Abstract: Capped-usage SaaS products -- LLM subscriptions such as Claude Code and ChatGPT, cloud platforms such as Vercel and Cloudflare Workers, corporate benefit platforms, identity-verification services with liability transfer -- share a structural signature with insurance products: a fixed premium decoupled from realized consumption, stochastic per-user demand with heavy-tailed severity, a non-fungible cap that resets on a fixed schedule, and a portfolio-level exposure that requires reserve adequacy under tail risk. We argue that this is not an analogy. It is the same operational problem actuarial science has been tooled for decades to address, restated with new dependent variables (tokens, bandwidth bytes, function-invocations, gym check-ins) in place of medical claims. This paper proposes a modeling framework for capped-usage SaaS pricing built from frequency-severity decomposition, premium calculation principles, and Monte Carlo reserve adequacy. We map the framework to publicly observable subscription tiers in two domains (LLM services and cloud platforms), ground it in canonical health-insurance economics (Arrow 1963; Pauly 1968; Manning et al. 1987; Brot-Goldberg et al. 2017), and demonstrate divergence from traditional unit economics through a worked example. The contribution is operational rather than theoretical: not a new theorem, but vocabulary and tools currently absent from cs.LG/stat.ML practice.

</details>


### [158] [Convex Dataset Valuation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16704)
*Siqi Zeng,Christopher Jung,Rui Li,Zhe Kang,Ming Li,Nima Noorshams,Zhigang Wang,Fuchun Peng,Han Zhao,Xue Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM后训练阶段的数据集估值问题，提出基于梯度空间核均值匹配（KMM）的可扩展凸优化方法，在预算约束下同时考虑数据集与目标任务的对齐性和数据集间的冗余性，实验证明该方法优于现有基线，计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，开发者面临计算、标注和许可成本约束，无法使用所有可用数据集进行后训练，因此需要原则性的数据集级选择方法。现有梯度对齐评分只考虑单数据集与目标的对齐，忽略了数据集间的冗余信息。

Method: 将数据集估值建模为子集选择问题，提出基于梯度空间核均值匹配（KMM）的凸优化估值方法，联合优化数据集与目标任务的分布对齐以及数据集间的冗余消除，方法可扩展且收敛性好。

Result: 在多种后训练设置和任务上的实验表明，该方法一致优于现有估值基线，在更强的性能下计算开销较低。

Conclusion: 数据集估值可作为市场约束下LLM后训练数据选择的实用决策工具，代码已开源。

Abstract: Improving LLM performance on downstream tasks sometimes requires leveraging auxiliary datasets during post-training. In practice, however, developers face constraints on compute, labeling, and licensing costs that preclude using all available data, necessitating principled dataset-level selection. These constraints are increasingly shaped by dataset marketplaces, where data acquisition is governed by budgets and negotiation. We study dataset valuation as a subset selection problem during LLM post-training. Our goal is to identify and weight auxiliary datasets so as to maximize target task performance given constrained budgets. We first show that commonly used gradient alignment scores provide a reasonable yet incomplete valuation signal, as they ignore redundancy among datasets. To address this, we propose a scalable convex dataset-level valuation method based on kernel mean matching (KMM) in gradient space, which jointly accounts for alignment with the target task and redundancy across auxiliary datasets. Through extensive experiments across diverse post-training settings and tasks, we show that our approach consistently outperforms existing valuation baselines, achieving stronger performance with low computational overhead. Our results position dataset valuation as a practical decision tool for post-training data selection in market-constrained large language model settings. The code is available at https://github.com/uiuctml/convex_data_valuation.

</details>


### [159] [EmoMind: Decoding Affective Captions from Human Brain fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16739)
*Bilal A. Mohammed,Lin Gu,Ruogo Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmoMind是首个从fMRI信号直接解码情感描述词的全流程pipeline，通过连续34维情感向量控制文本生成，在情感保真度和主体特异性上显著优于GPT-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑到文本系统仅恢复语义内容而丢弃情感信息，且语言模型使用离散情感标签无法捕捉个体间丰富的情感差异。

Method: 从fMRI解码视觉特征获取语义场景描述，再用同一fMRI记录的34维连续情感向量改写描述；使用无分类器引导训练改写器，平衡内容保留与情感表达。

Result: 在两个独立情感fMRI数据集上，EmoMind在所有三个评估轴（主体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）上均显著优于GPT-4，在个体情感结构指标上提升最大。

Conclusion: 连续脑解码情感可作为个性化情感描述生成的有效控制信号，为研究个体情感脑组织提供新方向。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from brain activity has advanced substantially, but cur- rent brain-to-text systems largely recover semantic content while discarding affect. Additionally, language models can generate emotional text when prompted with categorical labels, but such labels collapse rich inter-subject variability into coarse discrete bins. We present EmoMind, the first end-to-end pipeline for decoding affective captions directly from fMRI signals. EmoMind first retrieves a semanti- cally grounded neutral scene description from brain-decoded visual features, then rewrites it using a continuous 34-dimensional emotion vector decoded from the same fMRI recording. To control the balance between content preservation and affective expression, we train the rewriter with classifier-free guidance against an identity-preserving null branch, enabling smooth interpolation between semantic fidelity and affective expressivity. We evaluate affective caption generation with a three-axis validation framework spanning subject-specificity, structural geometry, and causal control. We further augment this framework with a synthetic-brain substitution test that probes robustness to the measurement apparatus, and we benchmark each axis against GPT-4 prompted with brain-decoded top-5 emotion labels as a strong discrete baseline. Across two independent emotion fMRI datasets, EmoMind significantly outperforms label-prompted GPT-4 on all three axes, with the largest gains on metrics that require person-specific affective structure rather than population-level emotion aggregation. These results establish continuous brain-decoded affect as a viable control signal for individualized affective cap- tion generation and open new directions for studying individual affective brain organisation.

</details>


### [160] [Propagation of Chaos in Contextual Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16747)
*Shi Chen,Zhengjiang Lin,Kaizhao Liu,Philippe Rigollet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了上下文流图（CFMs）的抽象框架，用于在大型上下文场景下对Transformer进行定量统计理论分析，建立了有限上下文与无限上下文系统之间在前向传播和训练轨迹两个维度的偏差控制界限，并达到了最优的Wasserstein率n^{-1/d}和参数率n^{-1/2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型上下文场景下，Transformer的理论理解尚不充分，特别是上下文长度n作为统计资源如何影响模型行为的定量分析缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出上下文流图（CFMs）抽象框架，将有限上下文模型近似为无限上下文系统；利用McKean-Vlasov动力学结构和传播混沌的经典工具；推导出新的欧拉伴随公式用于损失梯度，并建立前向-伴随系统的稳定性估计。

Result: 建立了前向偏差界限（控制有限与无限上下文CFMs沿深度的偏差）和后向偏差界限（控制训练轨迹在在线梯度下降迭代中的偏差），分别达到n^{-1/d}和n^{-1/2}的最优收敛率。

Conclusion: 该理论为理解Transformer在大上下文场景下的行为提供了严格的统计基础，证明了上下文长度作为统计资源的有效性，并给出了最优收敛速率。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative statistical theory of transformers in the large-context regime by adopting the abstraction of contextual flow maps (CFMs): dynamical systems that evolve a distinguished token in the presence of a contextual measure across a stack of attention blocks. Within this framework, the finite-context model approximates an idealized infinite-context system in which the contextual measure is replaced by its underlying population, so that the context length $n$ becomes a statistical resource. Exploiting the McKean--Vlasov structure of the dynamics and the classical machinery of propagation of chaos, we establish a forward bound controlling the deviation between the finite- and infinite-context CFMs uniformly along depth, and a backward bound controlling the deviation between the corresponding training trajectories uniformly across iterations of online gradient descent. Both bounds achieve the optimal Wasserstein rate $n^{-1/d}$ for general CFMs and parametric rate $n^{-1/2}$ for a restricted class of CFMs that includes transformers as a special case. The analysis rests on a new Eulerian adjoint formulation of the loss gradient and stability estimates for the resulting forward--adjoint system, both of which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [161] [Learning Unbiased Permutations via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16755)
*Yimeng Min,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PermFlow是一个基于条件流匹配的框架，直接在具有单位行和列和的仿射子空间上操作，通过闭式切空间投影器保持约束，使用最近目标耦合处理多模态排列分布，解决了传统Sinkhorn方法在模糊情况下崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵正则化Sinkhorn的可微排列方法只能产生单一软化解，在存在歧义时崩溃，无法捕捉排列分布的多模态性。

Method: 提出PermFlow框架：(1) 直接在具有单位行和列和的仿射子空间上操作；(2) 使用闭式切空间投影器保持约束，无需迭代校正；(3) 采用最近目标耦合策略，将不同噪声初始化导向不同有效排列。

Result: 在模糊数字混合的视觉排序任务和对称线性分配问题上，PermFlow在明确输入上达到高精度，在模糊情况下能恢复两个有效排列，而Sinkhorn基线方法结构性失败。

Conclusion: PermFlow有效捕获了排列分布的多模态性，克服了Sinkhorn方法在模糊场景下的固有限制。

Abstract: Learning permutations is fundamental to sorting, ranking, and matching, but existing differentiable methods based on entropy-regularized Sinkhorn produce a single softened solution and collapse under ambiguity. We present PermFlow, a conditional flow matching framework that operates directly on the affine subspace of matrices with unit row and column sums. A closed-form tangent-space projector preserves these constraints exactly along every trajectory, by construction rather than through iterative correction, and a nearest-target coupling routes distinct noisy initializations toward distinct valid permutations. The result is a model that captures multimodal permutation distributions rather than collapsing them to a single mode. On a visual sorting task with blended-digit ambiguity and a symmetric linear assignment problem, PermFlow achieves high accuracy on unambiguous inputs and recovers both valid permutations under ambiguity, where Sinkhorn-based baselines structurally fail.

</details>


### [162] [Distinguishable Deletion: Unifying Knowledge Erasure and Refusal for Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16776)
*Puning Yang,Junchi Yu,Qizhou Wang,Philip Torr,Bo Han,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可区分删除（D²）范式，通过能量索引在潜在表征层面限制有害知识分布，并基于能量的遗忘对齐（EUA）方法实现更安全、更有效的LLM遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KD范式存在偏差删除问题（仅抑制特定token序列而非完整知识），DR范式存在有害知识重现风险（底层知识未被真正移除）。需要一种既能彻底删除知识又能安全拒绝有害请求的新范式。

Method: 提出D²范式：在潜在表征层面限制响应分布以擦除有害知识，同时将其与保留知识区分开来。引入能量索引量化知识存在性和分离程度，实现基于能量的遗忘对齐（EUA），包括训练时的能量边界遗忘和推理时的能量基拒绝机制。

Result: 数学和实证分析表明能量索引既准确又高效，EUA在大量实验中显著优于此前方法。

Conclusion: D²范式通过能量基方法有效解决了KD的偏差删除和DR的知识重现问题，为LLM安全部署提供了更优的遗忘方案。

Abstract: Mitigating sensitive and harmful outputs is fundamental to ensuring safe deployment of LLMs. Existing approaches typically follow two paradigms: Knowledge Deletion (KD), which erases undesirable information during training, and Distinguishable Refusal (DR), which steers models away from using sensitive knowledge during inference. Despite rapid progress, KD-based unlearning struggles with biased deletion due to suppressing specific token sequences as a substitute for complete knowledge removal, whereas DR-based unlearning risks the re-emergence of harmful knowledge because the underlying knowledge remains intact. To address these issues, we propose Distinguishable Deletion ($\mathrm{D^2}$), a paradigm that restricts the response distribution in the latent representation rather than specific tokens to erase undesirable knowledge, while distinguishing it from retained knowledge, enabling a refusal mechanism to handle unlearned inputs safely and coherently. To implement $\mathrm{D^2}$, we introduce an energy index that quantifies the presence of knowledge and the separation between unlearned and retained content. Mathematical and empirical analyses show that energy is both accurate and efficient, enabling Energy-based Unlearning Alignment (EUA) to enforce energy-boundary unlearning during training and apply an energy-based refusal mechanism at inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EUA significantly outperforms previous methods, indicating the superiority of $\mathrm{D^2}$. Our code is available at https://github.com/Puning97/EUA-for-LLM-Unlearning.

</details>


### [163] [Lever: Speculative LLM Inference on Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16786)
*Tuowei Wang,Fengzu Li,Yanfan Sun,Wei Gao,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lever系统，通过联合优化推测解码的三个阶段（草稿生成、验证、执行），在智能手机上实现Flash存储大模型的高效推理，相比基线方法延迟降低2.93倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型需用于手机交互应用，但高质量模型超出手机DRAM容量；Flash可存储更大模型但推理速度慢，现有推测解码方法未针对移动端I/O延迟和计算限制进行优化。

Method: Lever系统联合优化推测解码三阶段：1) 草稿阶段：使用I/O和计算感知的增益-成本目标构建token树；2) 验证阶段：通过早期退出预测修剪低价值分支，减少目标模型计算量；3) 执行阶段：在移动CPU-NPU硬件上高效映射推测执行以提升利用率。

Result: 相比Flash卸载推理基线，推理延迟平均降低2.93倍；相比传统推测解码，延迟降低1.50倍；缩小了Flash备份与内存驻留LLM推理之间的延迟差距。

Conclusion: Lever系统有效解决了移动端Flash存储大模型推理的延迟问题，通过针对移动硬件特性的联合优化，显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly needed for interactive mobile applications, but high-quality models exceed the limited DRAM available on smartphones. Flash storage can hold larger models, yet flash-backed inference is slow because autoregressive decoding repeatedly invokes the target model and incurs costly I/O. We observe that speculative decoding is a natural fit for this setting: a small draft model can remain in DRAM, while a larger flash-resident target model verifies multiple candidate tokens per invocation. However, existing methods assume server-class accelerators and fail to account for prolonged I/O latency, limited computation parallelism, and irregular speculation execution.
  We present Lever, an end-to-end system for efficient flash-backed LLM inference on smartphones. Lever jointly optimizes the three stages of speculative decoding under mobile constraints. For drafting, it builds token trees using an I/O- and compute-aware gain-cost objective. For verification, it prunes low-value branches through early-exit prediction to reduce target-model computation. For execution, it maps speculation efficiently across mobile CPU-NPU hardware to improve utilization. Comprehensive evaluations show that Lever reduces inference latency by an average of 2.93x over baseline flash-offloaded inference and 1.50x over conventional speculative decoding, narrowing the latency gap between flash-backed and memory-resident LLM inference.

</details>


### [164] [The Unlearnability Phenomenon in RLVR for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16787)
*Yulin Chen,He He,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RLVR训练中一个反直觉现象：模型在困难样本上存在“不可学习子集”，即使有正确采样也无法学习，根本原因在于这些样本的表示存在缺陷（梯度相似度低、推理模式不可泛化），且RL难以通过数据增强缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升LLM推理能力，但其学习动力学尚未被充分探索，尤其是模型在困难样本上的学习失败原因不明。

Method: 通过交叉样本梯度分析，发现不可学习样本存在低梯度相似性和不可泛化的推理模式；验证现有优化、采样技术和数据增强均无法解决该问题。

Result: 困难样本中存在不可学习子集；这些样本具有表示层面的根本缺陷（低梯度相似性）；RL框架下数据增强无法改善其梯度相似性。

Conclusion: 首次系统刻画了RLVR训练中的不可学习数据现象，揭示了当前RL方法在推理任务中的根本局限性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has proven effective in improving Large Language Model's (LLM) reasoning ability. However, the learning dynamics of RLVR remain underexplored. In this paper, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: among hard examples that the model initially struggles with, a substantial subset remains unlearnable even when correct rollouts are present. To understand the phenomenon, we first demonstrate that existing optimization and sampling techniques fail to resolve unlearnability. With cross-example gradient analysis, we show that unlearnable examples have fundamental representation issue, characterized by low gradient similarity with the rest of the examples and ungeneralizable reasoning patterns. We further show that representation flaws are difficult to mitigate in RL, as data augmentation does not improve gradient similarity. Our study provides the first systematic characterization of unlearnable data in RLVR training and reveals fundamental limitations in current RL approaches for reasoning tasks. Code and data are available at \url{https://github.com/yulinchen99/unlearnability-rlvr}.

</details>


### [165] [TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Execution Rewards for Multi-Step Tool Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16790)
*Anay Kulkarni,ChiaEn Lu,Dheeraj Mekala,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TIER奖励框架，通过函数模式和运行时执行而非标准轨迹提供密集反馈，在组合工具使用任务上实现超90%准确率，突破了轨迹监督奖励在深度超过4步时的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在工具使用的多步组合场景中难以扩展：基于结果的奖励反馈稀疏，而基于轨迹监督的奖励依赖标注参考解，会惩罚有效替代方案，限制了可扩展性。

Method: TIER从函数模式和运行时执行中提取监督信号，将奖励分解为格式有效性、模式遵从性、执行成功和答案正确性四个维度，为每个步骤提供密集、可解释的序列级反馈。

Result: 在DepthBench（1-6步组合基准）上，TIER在所有步骤上均达到>90%准确率，而轨迹监督奖励在超过4步后性能崩塌；在BFCL v3和NestFUL上也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: TIER通过允许多种有效执行路径获得奖励，自然支持多解策略并适应工具接口演化，消融实验验证了所有奖励组件的必要性，突显了多层次监督对组合推理的重要性。

Abstract: Tool use enables large language models to solve complex tasks through sequences of API calls, yet existing reinforcement learning approaches fail to scale to multi-step composition settings. Outcome-based rewards provide only sparse feedback, while trajectory-supervised rewards depend on annotated reference solutions, penalizing valid alternatives and limiting scalability. We propose TIER: Trajectory-Invariant Execution Rewards, a reward framework that derives supervision directly from function schemas and runtime execution, rather than from reference trajectories. The reward decomposes into format validity, schema adherence, execution success, and answer correctness, providing dense, interpretable sequence-level feedback derived from fine-grained verification of individual steps of tool use. This design allows any valid execution path to receive credit, naturally supporting multiple solution strategies and adapting to evolving tool interfaces. On DepthBench, a compositional benchmark stratified by depth (1 to 6 steps), TIER achieves >90% accuracy across steps, where trajectory-supervised rewards collapse beyond step-4. We further demonstrate consistent gains on benchmarks like BFCL v3 and NestFUL. Ablation studies confirm that all reward components are necessary, highlighting the importance of multi-level supervision for compositional reasoning.

</details>


### [166] [Cross-Domain Molecular Relational Learning: Leveraging Chemical Structure-Activity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16799)
*Peiliang Zhang,Jingling Yuan,Shiqing Wu,Mengqing Hu,Chao Che,Yongjun Zhu,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DisTrans模型，通过域对抗训练和结构-语义迁移差异优化，解决了跨域分子关系学习中分子结构与视觉图像间的域间差异问题，在跨域策略下优于16种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子关系学习方法局限于域内建模，存在"域封闭"效应，难以解释跨域交互机制，亟需跨域分子关系学习方法。

Method: 1) 基于子结构拓扑差异的梯度反转策略，学习域可分离的结构表示；2) 跨域表示引导机制，对齐源域和目标域的功能基团语义信息。

Result: 在两种典型跨域策略下，DisTrans优于16种基线方法，即使在域间差异显著时仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: DisTrans有效实现了分子结构与视觉图像的跨域自适应表示，解决了跨域分子关系学习的难题。

Abstract: Recent advances in molecular representation integrates molecular topological and visual modalities, opening new avenues for precise Molecular Relational Learning (MRL). Existing MRL methods focus on intra-domain modeling, and their inherent domain-closed effect limits applicability to molecular science, particularly in elucidating cross-domain interaction mechanisms. Consequently, the imperative for Cross-Domain Molecular Relational Learning has become increasingly pressing. Benefiting from structure-activity analysis, we propose the Domain Adversarial Training Network with Structural-Semantic Transfer Discrepancy (DisTrans) to optimize cross-domain adaptive representation for molecular structures and visual images. 1) We employ the gradient reversal strategy based on substructure topological discrepancies between domains to learn the domain dependence of molecular structures. This strategy guides the model to adapt to the structural adjacency patterns in the target domain, generating domain-separable structural representations. 2) We apply the cross-domain representation guidance mechanism to align the functional-group semantic information between the source and target domains, learning cross-domain consistency information. The experimental results in two typical cross-domain strategies demonstrate that DisTrans outperforms 16 baseline methods, maintaining satisfactory performance even under pronounced inter-domain discrepancy.

</details>


### [167] [Cross-modal Affinity-aligned Multimodal Learning Analytics for Predicting Student Collaboration Satisfaction in Game-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16806)
*Wen-Hsin Tsai,Chia-Ming Lee,Yuk-Ying Tung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AAMLA框架，通过CAMA模块（跨模态亲和力引导对齐）解决多模态协作学习中因模态退化导致的表征脆弱性问题，在50名中学生的实验中验证了其鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态协作学习环境中，眼动等单一模态在不同学生群体中信息量不稳定，导致隐式注意力融合方法产生脆弱的跨模态表征；亟需一种能自适应抑制无信息模态又不完全丢弃它们的对齐机制。

Method: 提出AAMLA框架，核心是CAMA模块：通过亲和矩阵显式建模模态间关系，结合对比学习强制跨模态一致性，实现自适应抑制无信息模态；同时使用模态特定投影层将面部动作单元、头部姿态、眼动和交互日志映射到统一语义空间。

Result: 在50名中学生EcoJourneys协作学习环境实验中，AAMLA在标准和模态退化条件下均优于单模态基线和先前的跨注意力方法；SHAP和t-SNE分析证实CAMA生成鲁棒、可解释的跨模态表征。

Conclusion: AAMLA框架有效解决了协作学习中的模态退化问题，为自动预测学生协作满意度提供了更鲁棒的多模态融合方案。

Abstract: Collaborative game-based learning environments offer rich opportunities for small-group knowledge construction, yet automatically predicting student collaboration satisfaction remains challenging. A critical barrier is modality degradation: in educational deployments, individual modalities such as eye gaze exhibit inconsistent informativeness across student cohorts, causing implicit attention-based fusion to produce brittle multimodal representations. We propose the Affinity-Aligned Multimodal Learning Analytics (AAMLA) framework, whose core contribution is the Cross-modal Affinity-guided Modality Alignment (CAMA) module, which explicitly models inter-modal relationships via affinity matrices and enforces cross-modal consistency through contrastive learning, enabling adaptive suppression of uninformative modalities without discarding them. AAMLA further applies modality-specific projection layers to map heterogeneous features, including facial action units, head pose, eye gaze, and interaction trace logs, into a unified semantic space prior to alignment. Experiments on 50 middle school students in the EcoJourneys collaborative learning environment demonstrate consistent improvements over unimodal baselines and prior cross-attention approaches under standard and modality degradation conditions, with SHAP and t-SNE analyses confirming that CAMA produces robust, interpretable cross-modal representations for student collaboration modeling.

</details>


### [168] [Informative Graph Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16809)
*Shen Han,Zhiyao Zhou,Jiawei Chen,Sheng Zhou,Canghong Jin,Hai Lin,Da Zhong Li,Bingde Hu,Can Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出InGSL方法，通过互信息指导同时考虑相似性和多样性来构建图边，在减少边数量的情况下提升图结构学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图结构学习方法虽然能优化节点连接，但往往导致边数量急剧膨胀，带来巨大的存储和计算开销。作者发现这是因为基于相似性的边构建主要连接高度相似的邻居，引入了大量结构冗余。

Method: 提出InGSL（信息性图结构学习方法），采用互信息指导的学习策略，在边构建中同时考虑相似性和多样性，可作为插件模块集成到现有GSL框架中。

Result: 在六种代表性GSL方法上的实验表明，InGSL在减少边数量的情况下实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入多样性和互信息指导，可以有效减少图结构学习中的边冗余，兼顾效率与性能。

Abstract: The quality of graph-structured data is fundamental to the success of modern graph analysis techniques such as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). However, real-world graph data is often suboptimal, suffering from issues such as noise and incomplete connections. Graph Structure Learning (GSL) has emerged as a promising technique that adaptively optimizes node connections. However, we observe that the effectiveness of GSL often comes at the cost of a dramatic expansion in edge count, resulting in significant storage and computational overhead.
  In this work, we reveal that this limitation stems from the prevalent use of similarity-based edge construction, which predominantly connects highly similar neighbors based on their embeddings, introducing substantial structure redundancy. To address this, we propose a novel Informative Graph Structure Learning method (InGSL), which jointly considers both similarity and diversity in edge construction by incorporating a mutual-information-guided learning strategy. Notably, InGSL serves as a plug-in module that can be seamlessly integrated into existing GSL frameworks. Through extensive experiments on six representative GSL methods, we demonstrate that InGSL achieves significant performance improvements at a reduced number of edges.

</details>


### [169] [Jacobian-Guided Anisotropic Noise Reshaping for Enhancing Representation Utility under Local Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16812)
*Youngmok Ha,Viktor Schlegel,Yidan Sun,Anil Anthony Bharath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于雅可比矩阵的任务感知局部差分隐私方法，通过将标准LDP的各向同性噪声重塑为各向异性分布，在保持隐私预算不变的前提下，显著提升任务相关子空间的数据效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LDP机制是任务无关的，对所有维度均匀注入噪声，导致数据效用严重下降。需要一种能在保持隐私保护强度不变的同时，减少对下游任务关键维度噪声影响的方法。

Method: 通过公开下游模型的雅可比矩阵识别任务关键子空间，沿这些维度选择性衰减噪声，将标准LDP的各向同性噪声重塑为各向异性分布，实现每维度隐私预算均匀但噪声影响异质化。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C（亮度损坏，严重程度5级）数据集上，当ε=7.5时，集成该方法使PrivUnit2和PrivUnitG的效用提升约20%。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于线性和非线性模型，能与现有LDP机制无缝集成，在保持隐私预算不变的情况下显著提升数据效用。

Abstract: While Local Differential Privacy (LDP) serves as a foundational primitive for distributed data collection, its stringent noise injection requirement often leads to severe degradation in data utility. This degradation stems from the task-agnostic nature of conventional LDP mechanisms, which inject noise uniformly across all dimensions regardless of their relative importance to the downstream objective. To address this issue, we propose a novel approach that mitigates noise in task-relevant subspaces of the data representation. Our method identifies task-critical subspaces via the Jacobian matrix of the public downstream model, selectively attenuates noise along those dimensions, and reshapes the isotropic noise of standard LDP into an anisotropic distribution. This method preserves the uniform per-dimension privacy budget while heterogeneously modulating noise impact across dimensions, thereby substantially enhancing data utility. Furthermore, our approach generalizes to both linear and non-linear models and integrates seamlessly with existing mechanisms. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C (Brightness corruption at the highest severity level 5) demonstrate that integrating our approach improves the utility of PrivUnit2 and PrivUnitG by approximately 20\% at $ε=7.5$. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/ymha/jacobian-anr-ldp}.

</details>


### [170] [Decoupling KL and Trajectories: A Unified Perspective for SFT, DAgger, Offline RL, and OPD in LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16826)
*Anhao Zhao,Haoran Xin,Yingqi Fan,Junlong Tong,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地解耦了LLM知识蒸馏中的两个正交维度（前缀来源和Token级KL方向），提出了四种有效的蒸馏目标函数，并通过大量实验揭示了精度-熵、质量-计算、精度-稳定性三个权衡关系，最终提出了KL混合策略和熵门控长度课程方法，在数学推理任务上显著提升了性能并降低了响应长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练中知识蒸馏的设计空间（特别是在与强化学习结合时）缺乏深入理解，现有的离策略蒸馏和在线策略蒸馏隐式地将前缀来源和Token级KL方向耦合在一起，这种耦合并非本质的，需要解耦以探索更优的蒸馏目标。

Method: 将序列级KL分解为两个正交维度：前缀来源（教师前缀 vs 学生前缀）和Token级KL方向（前向KL vs 反向KL），得到四种有效目标函数。通过梯度级恒等式建立与SFT、DAgger、离线RL蒸馏和在线策略蒸馏的联系。提出KL混合策略和熵门控长度课程方法。

Result: 揭示了三个权衡关系：KL方向导致精度-熵权衡，前缀来源导致质量-计算权衡，训练长度导致精度-稳定性权衡。KL混合策略在长序列蒸馏中需较大前向KL权重以防止熵坍塌和长度膨胀。熵门控长度课程在Avg@k和Pass@k上分别提升3.6和5.8个点，同时将平均响应长度减少约3倍。

Conclusion: 本文为设计推理蒸馏目标提供了系统框架和实用方法，能够有效平衡精度、多样性、计算效率和RL行为之间的关系。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is central to LLM post-training, yet its design space remains poorly understood, especially alongside reinforcement learning (RL). We show that the prevailing paradigms, off-policy distillation and on-policy distillation (OPD), implicitly couple two orthogonal choices: prefix source and token-level KL direction. This follows from decomposing sequence-level KL over autoregressive response distributions: forward KL pairs teacher prefixes with token-level forward KL, and reverse KL pairs student prefixes with token-level reverse KL. We argue this coupling is not intrinsic: decoupling the two axes yields four valid objectives. We establish gradient-level identities showing forward KL gives SFT-style cross-entropy matching with teacher soft targets, whereas reverse KL gives an RL-style policy-gradient objective with a dense teacher-student log-ratio reward, connecting them to off-policy SFT, DAgger-style on-policy SFT, offline-RL-style distillation, and OPD. We conduct an extensive controlled study on math reasoning, evaluating the four objectives both as standalone methods and as initializations for subsequent RL. The results reveal three tradeoffs: KL direction induces an accuracy-entropy tradeoff, prefix source a quality-compute tradeoff, and training length an accuracy-stability tradeoff. Motivated by these findings, we propose KL mixing and an entropy-gated length curriculum. KL mixing shows long-sequence distillation requires substantial forward-KL weight to prevent entropy collapse and length inflation without sacrificing accuracy. The entropy-gated length curriculum improves Avg@k and Pass@k by 3.6 and up to 5.8 points, and cuts average response length by roughly 3x versus fixed long-horizon training. Our results provide a framework and practical methods for designing reasoning distillation objectives that balance accuracy, diversity, compute, and RL behavior.

</details>


### [171] [PhysioSeq2Seq: A Hybrid Physiological Digital Twin and Sequence-to-Sequence LSTM for Long-Horizon Glucose Forecasting in Type 1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16860)
*Phat Tran,Neville Mehta,Clara Mosquera-Lopez,Robert H. Dodier,Lizhong Chen,Peter G. Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhysioSeq2Seq混合架构，通过患者匹配的生理模型结合Seq2Seq LSTM，消除递归误差累积，在240分钟血糖预测上MAE为39.28 mg/dL，比递归LSTM降低偏倚13.89 mg/dL，比ODE数字孪生降低MAE 28.62 mg/dL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准递归LSTM在长时程血糖预测中存在系统性负偏倚（误差累积），而纯机理ODE模型在人群水平参数化时无法跨个体泛化，亟需一种能兼顾个体化生理约束与深度学习表达能力的混合方法。

Method: 构建PhysioSeq2Seq混合架构：1）为每个血糖片段通过孪生匹配从300个参数化数字孪生中选出最佳匹配个体；2）将匹配孪生的10个内部ODE状态变量作为外源协变量注入Seq2Seq LSTM的编码器和解码器；3）采用48步同时预测策略消除递归误差累积。

Result: 在T1DEXI数据集（348训练/74测试）上，240分钟预测MAE为39.28 mg/dL，ME为-10.62 mg/dL，相比递归LSTM偏倚降低13.89 mg/dL，相比ODE数字孪生MAE降低28.62 mg/dL。

Conclusion: 消除架构反馈并注入患者匹配的生理状态是面向1型糖尿病长时程血糖预测的有效且具有临床意义的策略。

Abstract: Accurate long-horizon glucose forecasting is critical for automated insulin delivery systems, which help people with type 1 diabetes (T1D) manage their glucose and avoid dangerous hypoglycemia. However, standard recursive long short-term memory (LSTM) networks suffer from systematic negative bias at longer horizons due to error compounding, while purely mechanistic ordinary differential equation (ODE) models fail to generalize across individuals when parameterized at the population level. We propose PhysioSeq2Seq, a hybrid architecture that combines patient-specific physiological modeling with a sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) LSTM. For each glucose segment, twin matching searches a population of 300 parameterized digital twins to identify the best-fitting physiological match from a 3-hour continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) history. The 10 internal ODE state variables of the matched twin are injected as exogenous covariates into both the encoder and decoder of the Seq2Seq LSTM. This simultaneous 48-step prediction strategy eliminates recursive error compounding, while the ODE features provide a physics-grounded constraint that bounds long-horizon drift within physiologically plausible ranges. PhysioSeq2Seq was trained on CGM and insulin data from 348 participants in the Type 1 Diabetes Exercise Initiative (T1DEXI) dataset and evaluated on 74 held-out participants. At the 240-minute horizon, PhysioSeq2Seq achieves a mean absolute error of 39.28 mg/dL and a mean error of -10.62 mg/dL, reducing bias by 13.89 mg/dL over the recursive LSTM and reducing mean absolute error by 28.62 mg/dL over the ODE-based digital twin. These results show that eliminating architectural feedback and injecting patient-matched physiological states is an effective and clinically meaningful strategy for long-horizon glucose forecasting in T1D.

</details>


### [172] [AIM: Adversarial Information Masking for Faithfulness Evaluation of Saliency Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16905)
*Chia-Ying Hsieh,Hsin-Yuan Fang,Chun-Shu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIM框架，通过对抗特征替换替代传统掩码来更可靠地评估显著性 faithfulness，并减少掩码偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后验显著性方法评估存在掩码混淆偏差（零掩码产生分布外伪影，插值掩码保留残余信息），难以可靠评估 faithfulness。

Method: 提出AIM框架：用输入的对抗样本值替换选中特征，结合互补掩码顺序评估；同时引入随机归因偏差和稳定性指标评估掩码算子可靠性。

Result: 在图像、音频和EEG任务上，AIM相比零掩码和插值掩码减少了掩码引入的偏差，并揭示了符号/无符号归因的模态依赖差异。

Conclusion: AIM能更可靠地评估显著性 faithfulness，且对不同模态下解释方法的可靠性提供了新见解。

Abstract: Post-hoc saliency methods are widely used to interpret deep neural networks, but their faithfulness is difficult to evaluate reliably. Existing evaluations mask features according to saliency-induced feature ordering and measure performance degradation, but this degradation can be confounded by the masking operator: zero masking may create out-of-distribution artifacts, while interpolation-based masking may preserve residual predictive information. We propose Adversarial Information Masking (AIM), a saliency-guided adversarial feature replacement framework for evaluating both saliency-map faithfulness and masking-operator reliability. AIM replaces selected features with values from an adversarial counterpart of the input and compares degradation under complementary masking orders. We assess reliability using random-attribution bias and stability of explanation-method faithfulness rankings. Experiments on image, audio, and EEG tasks suggest that AIM reduces masking-induced bias compared with zero and interpolation-based masking, while revealing modality-dependent differences between signed and unsigned attributions.

</details>


### [173] [Decision-Aware Proximal Bridge Learning for Optimal Treatment Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16989)
*Tomàs Garriga,Alejandro Almodóvar,Axel Brando,Gerard Sanz,Eduard Serrahima de Cambra,Juan Parras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向决策的加权桥损失函数，用于在存在隐混淆的因果推断中实现连续行动空间下的最优个体化治疗选择，并证明其能有效控制治疗选择遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近端因果推断方法主要关注治疗效果估计而非治疗选择优化，且标准估计目标根据观测治疗分布分配建模努力，而非决策相关区域，导致次优决策。

Method: 提出策略目标加权桥损失函数，强调决策相关治疗区域同时保持全局稳定性；设计交替迭代算法，包含加权桥估计、响应面投影、策略更新和权重细化四个步骤。

Result: 理论证明该损失函数通过加权不适定性常数控制治疗选择遗憾；实验表明决策感知加权在多个桥求解器上均能降低遗憾。

Conclusion: 决策感知加权桥损失可有效改善近端因果推断中的治疗选择性能，填补了该领域在最优决策方面的空白。

Abstract: Individualized treatment selection with continuous actions requires accurate causal response estimation in decision-relevant regions, rather than uniformly over the entire action space. Estimating a global causal response surface and then choosing the treatment that maximizes it can therefore be suboptimal, since standard estimation objectives allocate modeling effort according to the observed treatment distribution rather than the regions that determine the optimal decision. While decision-aware approaches have been studied in unconfounded settings, this problem remains underexplored in proximal causal inference, where proxy variables and bridge functions enable identification under suitable assumptions even in the presence of hidden confounding. Despite recent progress, proximal methods have primarily focused on treatment-effect and potential-outcome estimation rather than treatment selection and optimal decision-making. To bridge this gap, we introduce a policy-targeted weighted bridge loss that emphasizes decision-relevant treatment regions while retaining global stabilization. We prove a regret bound showing that the proposed weighted bridge loss controls treatment-selection regret through a weighted ill-posedness constant. We instantiate the framework in decision-aware variants of several proximal bridge solvers, yielding practical algorithms that alternate between weighted bridge estimation, response-surface projection, policy update, and weight refinement. Empirically, we find that decision-aware weighting reduces regret across several bridge solvers, suggesting improved treatment selection in proximal settings.

</details>


### [174] [When Dynamics Shift, Robust Task Inference Wins: Offline Imitation Learning with Behavior Foundation Models Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17017)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Rahul Jain,Ashutosh Nayyar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒行为基础模型（Robust BFM），通过在任务推理阶段引入最小最大优化，使预训练的BFM能够适应环境动力学变化（如摩擦、驱动噪声等），而无需重新预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFM假设固定环境动力学，在真实世界中面对摩擦、驱动、传感器噪声等动态变化时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将BFM任务推理形式化为鲁棒最小最大优化问题，在仅使用单一标称环境的离线数据条件下，实现对最坏情况动力学扰动的自适应。

Result: 在动力学变化下显著优于标准BFM和鲁棒离线IL基线方法。

Conclusion: 首次在仅依赖离线数据且不修改预训练的条件下，在任务推理阶段实现BFM对动力学偏移的鲁棒性，提升了BFM在动态环境中的实用性。

Abstract: Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) enable scalable imitation learning (IL) by pretraining task-agnostic representations that can be rapidly adapted to new tasks. However, existing BFMs assume fixed environment dynamics, limiting their robustness under real-world shifts such as changes in friction, actuation, or sensor noise. We address this by formulating BFM task-inference as a robust minimax optimization problem, enabling adaptation to worst-case dynamics perturbations without modifying pretraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first BFM-based framework that achieves robustness to dynamics shifts while relying solely on offline data from a single nominal environment. Our approach significantly outperforms standard BFM and robust offline IL baselines under dynamics shifts. These results demonstrate that robust policy can be achieved entirely at task-inference time, improving the practicality of BFMs in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [175] [Why Do Reasoning Models Lose Coverage? The Role of Data and Forks in the Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17026)
*Ngoc-Hieu Nguyen,Parshin Shojaee,Phuc Minh Nguyen,Nan Zhang,Chandan K Reddy,Khoa D Doan,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了推理模型在SFT后训练中出现的"覆盖收缩"现象（pass@k下降），发现其与训练数据中的"决策点"（forking paths）场景密切相关，并提出了通过针对性数据合成和多样性解码机制来缓解的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在SFT后训练中虽然提升了pass@1准确率，但出现了pass@k退化的覆盖收缩行为，作者希望探究这一现象的根本原因。

Method: 通过设计受控实验（图分支中的不可判读节点、推理模式等决策点场景），追踪后训练动态，分析收缩现象与训练数据中决策点场景 prevalence 的关联，并尝试通过目标数据合成和多样性解码机制进行缓解。

Result: 发现收缩现象与训练数据中决策点场景的出现频率高度相关；通过针对性数据合成和多样性鼓励解码机制可部分缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 数据中心因素是推理模型收缩的关键驱动因素，多样性感知设计是控制该问题的有效手段。

Abstract: Recent progress in large language models has led to the emergence of reasoning models, which have shown strong performance on complex tasks through specialized fine-tuning procedures. While these methods reliably improve pass@1 accuracy, prior works have observed that they show a coverage shrinkage behavior, where pass@k degrades relative to the base model. In this paper, we investigate the reasoning shrinkage arise under SFT-based post-training. We hypothesize that this behavior is driven by properties of the fine-tuning data, specifically related to decision points or "forks in the road" scenarios where model faces indecipherable patterns with multiple valid reasoning paths. To test this hypothesis, we design controlled case studies that simulate such decision-point settings, spanning indecipherable nodes in graph branching, and reasoning modes. By tracking post-training dynamics in these settings, we find that the shrinkage phenomenon is tightly correlated with the prevalence of decision-point scenarios in the training data. We also demonstrate that this shrinkage behavior can be partially mitigated through targeted data synthesis design of decision-points, and a more systematic diversity-encouraging decoding mechanism. Our findings identify data-centric factors as a key driver of shrinkage in reasoning models and highlight diversity-aware designs as an effective lever for controlling it.

</details>


### [176] [Privacy Policy Enforcement Guardrails for Data-Sensitive Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17034)
*Osama Zafar,Alexander Nemecek,Yiqian Zhang,Wenbiao Li,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双单类密度估计器和融合文本嵌入的隐私策略执行框架（T3+OCSVM），用于检测RAG系统中非结构化属性簇导致的上下文数据泄露，在医学、金融和法律领域的压力测试中实现0.93+的AUROC，并将误报率降低44-55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PII过滤器常遗漏RAG系统中非监管属性簇（如非敏感字段组合）导致的上下文数据泄露，现有高斯混合基线在边界安全压力测试中因关注语言风格而非内容而失效。

Method: 采用双单类密度估计器（OCSVM）与融合文本嵌入，结合校准的弃权区域处理分布外输入；利用轴分层、多LLM合成数据流水线生成医学、金融和法律领域的训练数据。

Result: T3+OCSVM在边界安全场景下AUROC达0.93以上，误报率降低44-55个百分点，保持毫秒级延迟；相较于监督MLP分类器（高弃权率）和14B参数LLM裁判（高延迟和校准问题）具有更优的操作适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为任何基于合成数据训练的分类器提供了稳健的压力测试标准，在隐私策略执行中兼顾了高精度、低误报和实时性。

Abstract: Standard PII filters often miss contextual data leakage in RAG systems, such as non-regulated attribute clusters that collectively identify individuals. We introduce a Privacy Policy Enforcement (PPE) framework using dual one-class density estimators with fused text embeddings and a calibrated abstain region for out-of-distribution inputs. Using an axis-stratified, multi-LLM synthetic data pipeline across medicine, finance, and law, we found that traditional Gaussian Mixture baselines fail on borderline-safe stress tests by focusing on linguistic register rather than content.
  Our proposed T3+OCSVM detector, trained on safe and borderline-safe data, achieves a borderline AUROC of 0.93+ while reducing false positives by 44-55 percentage points and maintaining millisecond latency. Compared to supervised MLP classifiers or 14B-parameter LLM judges, our framework offers superior operational suitability, as the former suffers from high abstention rates and the latter from latency and calibration issues. This methodology provides a robust stress-testing standard for any synthetic-data-trained classifier.

</details>


### [177] [D$^2$Evo: Dual Difficulty-Aware Self-Evolution for Data-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17037)
*Ru Zhang,Renda Li,Ziyu Ma,Weijie Qiu,Chongyang Tao,Yong Wang,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D²Evo框架，通过双难度感知的自进化强化学习，仅用不到2000个数学样本就能有效提升大语言模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习训练LLM面临有效数据稀缺和难度动态变化两大挑战，现有方法存在无锚点生成、忽略共同进化、难度不匹配等问题。

Method: D²Evo框架：每轮迭代中基于当前求解器能力挖掘中等难度锚点，训练提问器生成适当难度的多样化问题，并联合优化两个组件以实现渐进式推理提升。

Result: 在数学推理基准上超越现有方法（仅需少于2000个真实数学样本），并在通用推理基准上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 双难度感知的自进化RL框架能有效解决数据稀缺和难度偏移问题，实现高效推理能力提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated potential for enhancing reasoning in large language models (LLMs). However, effective RL training, which requires medium-difficulty training samples, faces two fundamental challenges: Effective Data Scarcity and Dynamic Difficulty Shifts, where medium-difficulty samples are scarce and become trivial as models improve. Existing methods mitigate this scarcity to some extent by generating training samples. However, these approaches suffer from anchor-free generation, ignoring co-evolution, and difficulty mismatch. To address these issues, we propose D$^2$Evo, a Dual Difficulty-aware self-Evolution RL framework. In each iteration, our method mines medium-difficulty anchors based on the current Solver's capability, trains the Questioner to generate diverse questions at appropriate difficulty levels, and jointly optimizes both components to enable progressive reasoning gains. Extensive experiments demonstrate that D$^2$Evo outperforms existing methods on mathematical reasoning benchmarks with fewer than 2K real mathematical samples, and exhibits strong generalization on general reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [Privacy-Preserving Generation Fraud Detection for Distributed Photovoltaic Systems: A Solar Irradiance-Fused Federated Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17039)
*Xiaolu Chen,Chenghao Huang,Yanru Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联邦学习的隐私保护分布式光伏发电欺诈检测框架，通过协同注意力机制融合光伏发电与天气数据，利用原型对齐解决类别不平衡问题，在真实数据集上验证了其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 住宅光伏系统广泛采用带来了发电欺诈检测的新挑战，传统方法聚焦用电侧，而光伏发电的间歇性、不确定性和分布式特性使集中式检测面临可扩展性与隐私问题。

Method: 提出联邦学习框架：每个社区部署本地检测器，通过协同注意力机制融合光伏发电与天气数据；联邦学习实现跨社区参数与原型聚合，利用原型对齐增强欺诈样本表征以解决类别不平衡。

Result: 在真实住宅光伏数据集上的实验表明，该方法在各种场景下均优于最先进的联邦学习方法，具有良好的可扩展性，并对类别不平衡具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的隐私保护分布式框架能有效检测光伏发电欺诈，兼顾可扩展性、隐私性与类别不平衡问题，为分布式光伏场景下的欺诈检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The wide adoption of residential photovoltaic (PV) systems introduces new challenges for generation fraud detection (FD). Unlike traditional electricity theft detection, which focuses on electricity consumption-side behavior, PV generation fraud detection (PVG-FD) is complicated by the inherent intermittency and uncertainty of PV generation. The distributed nature of PV systems poses further challenges for centralized PVG-FD approaches due to scalability and privacy concerns. This paper develops a privacy-preserving distributed PVG-FD framework based on federated learning (FL). In this framework, a utility company manages multiple household communities, where each of which is equipped with a local detector. The framework integrates a novel detection model architecture with privacy-preserving global collaboration. Each community's local model fuses PV generation and weather data via a co-attention mechanism to detect discrepancies critical for PVG-FD. The FL framework enables cross-community collaboration by aggregating model parameters and prototypes, leveraging global knowledge sharing with local refinement while preserving privacy. It also uses prototype alignment to address class imbalance by enhancing fraud sample representation. Extensive experiments on a real-world residential PV dataset validate the effectiveness of the developed method and demonstrate that it outperforms state-of-the-art FL methods across various scenarios. The results also show its scalability across varying community sizes and strong robustness to class imbalance.

</details>


### [179] [S-Bus: Automatic Read-Set Reconstruction for Multi-Agent LLM State Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17076)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-Bus 是一个 HTTP 中间件，通过 DeliveryLog 机制提供 Observable-Read Isolation (ORI) 一致性，自动检测并防止多智能体协作中的结构性竞态条件（写-写冲突和跨分片脏读），无需修改智能体 SDK，经形式化验证和实验证明与 PostgreSQL SERIALIZABLE 同等有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体框架（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen）对共享的可变自然语言状态没有写所有权语义，导致结构性竞态条件（SRCs）——写-写冲突和跨分片脏读——无声地破坏智能体输出。

Method: 提出 S-Bus，一个 HTTP 中间件，核心是服务器端 DeliveryLog：每个智能体记录 HTTP GET 操作日志，在提交时自动重建读取集。提供 Observable-Read Isolation (ORI)——一种基于 HTTP 可观测投影的部分因果一致性。通过 TLAPS 机器检验、TLC 穷举模型检测和 Dafny 归纳引理三层形式化验证。

Result: (1) 形式化验证：TLAPS 验证 ReadSetSoundness 和 ORICommitSafety，TLC 在 N=3 下枚举 20,763,484 个状态无违规，Dafny 证明 9 个引理。(2) 实证：与 PostgreSQL 17 SERIALIZABLE 和 Redis 7 WATCH/MULTI 对比，427,308 个 HTTP-409 冲突中零 Type-I 损坏。(3) 适用范围：专用分片工作负载下语义中立，单分片协作写入时因保留传播并发矛盾而有害。

Conclusion: S-Bus 的 DeliveryLog 机制在 HTTP 层面有效防止多智能体结构性竞态条件，与现有数据库隔离级别等价，但适用范围受限于拓扑条件。

Abstract: Concurrent LLM agents sharing mutable natural-language state produce Structural Race Conditions (SRCs): write-write and cross-shard stale-read conflicts that silently corrupt agent output. Existing multi-agent frameworks (LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen) provide no write-ownership semantics over shared state.
  We present S-Bus, an HTTP middleware whose central mechanism is a server-side DeliveryLog: a per-agent log of HTTP GET operations that automatically reconstructs each agent's read set at commit time without agent SDK changes under HTTP/1.1. The consistency property the DeliveryLog provides -- Observable-Read Isolation (ORI), a partial causal consistency over the HTTP-observable projection of the read set -- prevents structural race conditions when agents collaborate via shared shards.
  Three contributions: (C1) The DeliveryLog mechanism for automatic HTTP-traffic-based read-set reconstruction, with three-tier mechanised evidence: ReadSetSoundness and ORICommitSafety machine-checked in TLAPS (modulo one retained typing axiom); exhaustive TLC at N=3 (20,763,484 distinct states, zero violations); Dafny discharges 9 inductive soundness lemmas. (C2) Empirical structural-conflict prevention parity against PostgreSQL 17 SERIALIZABLE and Redis 7 WATCH/MULTI on shared-shard contention sweeps with 427,308 active HTTP-409 conflicts: zero Type-I corruptions across all three backends. (C3) ORI's operating envelope is topology-conditional: semantically neutral in dedicated-shard workloads; harmful in single-shard collaborative writing because preservation propagates concurrent contradictions.
  Source code: https://github.com/sajjadanwar0/sbus

</details>


### [180] [Scale Determines Whether Language Models Organize Representation Geometry for Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17084)
*Weilun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Subspace PGA度量，发现语言模型中间层表征几何结构显著服务于预测目标，但模型规模决定这种组织方式：小模型在后期训练中逐渐丧失预测对齐，大模型则保持稳定；这种差异源于少数主导方向迁移掩盖了底层预测结构，而非破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具描述了表征空间的几何形状，但未探究这种形状的组织目的。本文旨在检验语言模型中间层距离结构是否服务于最终的预测（即与解嵌入矩阵W_U的读出子空间对齐）。

Method: 提出Subspace PGA度量，比较层内距离结构与W_U读出子空间的对齐程度（vs. 同等大小的随机子空间）。在Pythia系列（70M-6.9B）及三组跨家族模型上进行实验分析。

Result: 中等层几何显著组织于预测（峰值z=9-24）；小模型（d≤1024）在后期训练中逐渐失去对齐但损失持续下降；大模型（d≥2048）始终保持对齐。少数主导方向迁移"遮蔽"而非破坏底层预测结构，移除后对齐恢复。

Conclusion: 模型规模不仅决定预测能力，更决定表征几何如何组织以实现预测。光谱指标和损失曲线无法捕捉此差异，Subspace PGA揭示了规模依赖的几何-预测关系。

Abstract: In language models, what a representation encodes is determined by the geometry of its representation space: distances, not activations, carry meaning. Existing tools characterize the shape of this geometry but do not ask what that shape is organized for. We introduce Subspace PGA, a metric that tests whether a layer's distance structure aligns with the readout subspace of the unembedding matrix $W_U$ more than with random subspaces of equal size. Across seven Pythia models (70M--6.9B) and three cross-family models, intermediate geometry is significantly organized for prediction (peak $z = 9$--$24$), but the degree is scale-dependent: small models ($d \leq 1024$) progressively lose it at late layers during training -- even as loss keeps improving -- while large models ($d \geq 2048$) preserve it throughout. We trace this to a capacity trade-off: a few dominant directions migrate away from $W_U$'s readout, masking rather than destroying the predictive structure beneath, and removing them restores alignment. Neither spectral metrics nor loss curves capture this distinction. Scale thus determines not only how well a model predicts, but how its representation geometry is organized to do so.

</details>


### [181] [Mechanism Learning: Prototype-Anchored Mechanism Inference for Scientific Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17091)
*Qian Jiang,Liping Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机制学习（Mechanism Learning）框架，通过学习局部演化规则而非直接预测状态，在数据稀缺、长时域、非平稳和高维等脆弱条件下实现更鲁棒的预测。在Burgers、WeatherBench2、Lorenz96上验证，优于FNO、NODE、LSTM等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接状态预测在数据稀缺、长时域、非平稳动力学或高维复杂度下性能脆弱，而局部演化规则往往具有可重用性，因此需要一种更鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 将局部的时空片段压缩为机制描述子，构建数据驱动的结构化机制空间；利用原型锚点（一组代表性机制）稀疏覆盖局部规则空间，将估计锚定在观测数据上。

Result: 在Burgers动力学中显著提升切换稳定性；在WeatherBench2稀缺数据固定时域协议和Lorenz96中等复杂度场景下达到SOTA；消融实验证明改进源于有限原型锚点而非潜在容量。

Conclusion: 机制学习可作为直接状态预测的原则性、鲁棒性替代方案，在复杂系统预测中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Scientific forecasting typically relies on direct state prediction, an approach that grows brittle under data scarcity, extended horizons, non-stationary dynamics, or high-dimensional complexity. While raw state trajectories are highly sensitive in these regimes, underlying local evolution rules often exhibit robust reusability. We introduce mechanism learning, a framework that forecasts future states by estimating the currently active local mechanism. Our method compresses local spatiotemporal fragments into mechanism descriptors, forming a data-driven, structured mechanism space where proximity reflects similar local evolution rules. To ground these estimates in observed data, we utilize prototype anchors, a set of representative mechanisms that sparsely cover the space of local rules. We evaluate this approach on Burgers dynamics, WeatherBench2, and Lorenz96. Empirically, the learned mechanism spaces resist collapse and maintain strong local consistency. Compared to direct prediction and other models including FNO, NODE, LSTM, and reservoir-family methods, our framework demonstrates predictive gains in fragile regimes: it significantly improves switching stability in Burgers dynamics and achieves state-of-the-art performance both under the scarce-data fixed-horizon WeatherBench2 protocol and in intermediate-complexity Lorenz96. Ablation studies and drift diagnostics confirm that these improvements are driven by finite prototype anchoring rather than sheer latent capacity. Together, these results establish mechanism learning as a principled, robust alternative to direct state prediction in forecasting complex systems.

</details>


### [182] [Parallel Recursive LSTM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17108)
*Tristan Gaudreault,Yongyi Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出并行递归LSTM（PR-LSTM），通过平衡树结构进行递归非线性状态合并，将循环并行深度从线性降至对数，避免了注意力机制的二次复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的二次时间/内存成本在长上下文中效率低，而LSTM等循环模型因严格顺序计算限制了并行性。旨在保留非线性状态更新的表达能力的同时提升并行效率。

Method: 将token独立映射到隐状态，然后通过可学习的门控合并模块在平衡计算树上递归合并，采用并行扫描的归约模式作为固定执行调度，不要求关联性递推。

Result: 在形式语言基准上实现强序列长度泛化，解决了比标准RNN、LSTM和Transformer基线更多的任务，同时避免了注意力的二次缩放。

Conclusion: 循环计算可以通过层次化重组暴露并行性，而不必将转移动力学限制为线性或关联形式。

Abstract: Transformers have become the dominant architecture for sequence modeling by using self-attention to enable expressive and highly parallel processing. However, the resulting quadratic time and memory costs limit efficiency in long-context settings. Recurrent models such as LSTMs provide explicit nonlinear state updates and strong state-tracking capabilities, yet their strictly sequential computation limits parallelism. We introduce the Parallel Recursive LSTM (PR-LSTM), a hierarchical recurrent architecture that replaces left-to-right recurrence with recursive nonlinear state composition over a balanced computation tree. Tokens are first mapped independently to latent states, which are then recursively merged by a learned gated composition block. This structure uses the reduction pattern underlying parallel scans as a fixed execution schedule, rather than assuming an associative recurrence. As a result, PR-LSTM retains nonlinear gated state representations while reducing recurrent parallel depth from linear to logarithmic. Empirically, PR-LSTM achieves strong sequence-length generalization on formal-language benchmarks, solving more tasks than standard RNN, LSTM, and Transformer baselines, while avoiding the quadratic scaling of attention. These results suggest that recurrent computation can be reorganized hierarchically to expose parallelism without restricting the transition dynamics to linear or associative forms.

</details>


### [183] [DynMuon: A Dynamic Spectral Shaping View of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17109)
*Fangzhou Wu,Rikhav Shah,Sandeep Silwal,Qiuyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynMuon方法，通过动态调整谱整形参数p（从正值到轻微负值），在训练大语言模型时比Muon方法降低验证损失，并节省10.6-26.5%的训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon方法通过极因子分解替代标准梯度下降更新矩阵，在LLM训练中表现优异。作者希望进一步探索更新矩阵的谱整形操作，以理解如何根据损失曲率、随机梯度噪声和训练阶段来最优选择p值。

Method: 定义一类Muon类更新，将更新矩阵M替换为UΣ^p V^T（谱整形）。理论分析p的选择依赖于局部曲率、梯度噪声和训练阶段。基于理论洞察，提出DynMuon方法，在训练过程中动态调度p从正值逐渐过渡到轻微负值。

Result: DynMuon在多种模型规模、架构和训练设置下，始终比Muon获得更低的验证损失，并且达到相同目标损失所需步数减少10.6-26.5%。

Conclusion: 正p在训练早期通过强调高曲率方向加速信号收缩，轻微负p在训练后期将更新强度重新分配到仍包含有用信号的低曲率方向。动态调度p的策略是有效的。

Abstract: In recent years, Muon has emerged as the dominant method for training large language models, and transformers more broadly. The essential difference, when compared to standard gradient descent methods, is to replace the usual update matrix $M=UΣV^\top$ with its polar factor $UV^\top$. In this work, we consider a class of Muon-like updates, where we replace the update $M$ with $UΣ^p V^\top$ for some parameter $p$. We call this a "spectral-shaping" operation, and develop a theory of how to pick $p$ which depends on (a) local curvature of the loss function, (b) noise stemming from stochastic gradients and label noise, and (c) training stage. Our theory and experimentation reveal a previously overlooked behavior: positive $p$ helps early by emphasizing high-curvature directions and accelerating signal contraction, while mildly negative $p$ helps later by reallocating update strength toward low-curvature directions that still contain useful training signals. Building on the insight, we propose DynMuon, an efficient dynamic spectral shaping method that schedules $p$ from positive to mildly negative over training. Extensive experiments across model sizes, architectures, and training settings show that DynMuon consistently achieves lower validation loss than Muon, while requiring 10.6-26.5% fewer steps to reach the same target loss.

</details>


### [184] [An Analytical Multiple Criteria Framework for Temporal and Dynamic Business-to-Business Customer Segmentation in Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17151)
*Muhammad Raees,Konstantinos Papangelis,Vassilis Javed Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态多准则决策方法，通过扩展RFM模型、集成层次分析法和多变量时间序列聚类，实现了B2B制造业客户细分的决策支持，并在真实数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RFM模型在客户细分中标准有限，无法捕捉复杂的业务交互，需要更动态、多维度的细分方法以支持B2B制造业的决策。

Method: 1) 将RFM扩展至稳定性和增长维度；2) 集成自适应分析层次过程以匹配业务目标；3) 评估多变量时间序列聚类模型；4) 测量客户稳定性、追踪区间迁移和波动性；5) 应用基于图的共识模型进行强化分析。

Result: 在真实制造业数据集的3000多个B2B客户上验证，该方法对时间偏移具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法为领域专家提供了偏好分析工具，支持其制定策略，为B2B客户细分提供了有效的决策支持。

Abstract: In sales and marketing, customer segmentation is an important tool for formulating strategies for customer treatment and supply chain management. Most segmentation implementations rely on limited criteria, such as recency, frequency, and monetary (RFM) modeling, which often fail to capture complex business interactions. In this work, we design and evaluate a dynamic multi-criteria decision-making (MCDM) method in a business-to-business (B2B) manufacturing context by 1) extending RFM to dimensions of stability and growth, 2) integrating an adaptive and analytical hierarchical process to match business objectives, and 3) evaluating multivariate time-series clustering models. We then measure customer stability, tracking between-segment transitions, and volatility over time, and apply a graph-based consensus model to further strengthen the analysis. We test the efficacy of the proposed method using a real-world manufacturing company dataset to segment more than 3,000 B2B customers, showing strong robustness to temporal shifts. The implementation enables domain experts with preferential analytics to devise their strategies, providing effective decision support for B2B customer segmentation.

</details>


### [185] [When Bits Break Recourse: Counterfactual-Faithful Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17160)
*Chaymae Yahyati,Ismail Lamaakal,Khalid El Makkaoui,Ibrahim Ouahbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量化会导致反事实可解释性失效的问题，并设计了CFQ方法在保持精度的同时维护反事实稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化在低比特部署下能保持预测精度，但会无声地破坏算法的反事实可解释性（即量化前后的决策翻转不一致或成本大幅增加），传统评估仅关注准确率无法发现此问题。

Method: 形式化了反事实对量化的三种稳定性（有效性、成本、方向），提出VD和CRG两个指标；并设计了CFQ方法，通过训练量化参数和混合精度位分配，在全局比特预算下强制保留教师模型的反事实行为。

Result: 在Adult、German Credit、COMPAS数据集上，精度匹配的基线方法会显著降低反事实稳定性，而CFQ在保持精度的同时大幅改善了VD和CRG指标。

Conclusion: 量化对反事实可解释性的破坏是现有精度指标无法捕捉的，CFQ能有效兼顾精度与反事实保真度。

Abstract: Quantization can preserve predictive accuracy under low-bit deployment while silently breaking algorithmic recourse: an actionable change that flips a decision before quantization may fail after quantization, or become substantially more costly. We formalize counterfactual sensitivity under quantization through validity, cost, and direction stability, and introduce two metrics: Validity Drop (VD) and Counterfactual Recourse Gap (CRG) that reveal recourse failures invisible to accuracy. We propose Counterfactual-Faithful Quantization (CFQ), which trains quantizer parameters and mixed-precision bit allocation to preserve counterfactual behavior by enforcing the target outcome at teacher recourse points under a global bit budget. A margin-based analysis gives a sufficient condition for recourse transfer under bounded quantization perturbations. Experiments on Adult, German Credit, and COMPAS show that accuracy-matched baselines can significantly degrade recourse stability, while CFQ maintains accuracy and substantially improves VD and CRG across bit budgets.

</details>


### [186] [TriAxialKV: Toward Extreme Low-Precision KV-Cache Quantization for Agentic Inference Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17170)
*Hanzhang Shen,Haoran Wu,Yiren Zhao,Robert Mullins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TriAxialKV是一种面向Agent工作负载的三轴混合精度KV缓存量化方案，通过时间、模态和语义角色三个维度分配INT2/INT4位宽，在OSWorld基准测试中达到BF16精度、4.5倍KV缓存压缩和30%吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化方法通常同质化处理或仅考虑单一维度（如时间邻近性或模态）的异构性，忽略了Agent工作负载中上下文沿时间、模态和语义角色三个关键轴的结构化重要性差异。

Method: 提出TriAxialKV方案：为每个token分配三轴标签，校准每个标签的敏感度，在固定内存预算下分配INT2/INT4位宽；实现包含校准、混合精度量化和内存管理的端到端服务系统，以及自定义融合Triton解码内核。

Result: 在OSWorld基准测试中使用Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking作为计算机使用Agent，达到SGLang BF16 KV缓存的精度，同时实现4.5倍KV缓存压缩和30%端到端吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: TriAxialKV通过三轴混合精度量化有效利用了Agent工作负载中上下文的结构化异构性，在保持精度的同时显著提升了KV缓存效率和系统吞吐量。

Abstract: Agentic workloads have emerged as a major workload for LLM inference. They differ significantly from chat-only workloads, requiring long-context processing, the ability to handle multimodal inputs, and structured multi-turn interactions with tool calling capabilities. As a result, their context exhibits structure that can carry different importance along three key axes: temporal recency to the current turn, modality such as text or image tokens, and semantic role such as user queries, tool calls, observations, or reasoning. These axes capture distinct token behaviors and lead to different sensitivities to KV-cache compression. However, existing KV-cache quantization methods are typically homogeneous or exploit only heterogeneity on a single dimension, such as temporal proximity or modality, overlooking the interactions among them. To this end, we introduce TriAxialKV, a novel mixed-precision KV-cache quantization scheme that assigns each token a triaxial tag, calibrates per-tag sensitivity, and allocates INT2/INT4 bitwidths under a fixed memory budget. We implement TriAxialKV as an end-to-end serving system, comprising calibration, mixed-precision quantization and memory management, and custom fused Triton decode kernels. When using Qwen3-VL-32B-Thinking as a computer-use agent operating the OSWorld, TriAxialKV matches the accuracy of SGLang with BF16 KV cache while supporting 4.5$\times$ KV cache size and achieving 30% higher end-to-end throughput, when running on real GPU systems.

</details>


### [187] [OpenJarvis: Personal AI, On Personal Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17172)
*Jon Saad-Falcon,Avanika Narayan,Robby Manihani,Tanvir Bhathal,Herumb Shandilya,Hakki Orhun Akengin,Gabriel Bo,Andrew Park,Matthew Hart,Caia Costello,Chuan Li,Christopher Ré,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OpenJarvis 是一种分解式个人AI堆栈架构，通过将系统表示为五个独立可编辑原语（智能、引擎、智能体、工具与记忆、学习）的类型规范，并引入LLM引导的规范搜索实现本地-云协作，使得本地模型在保持隐私优势的同时，在8个基准测试中的4个上达到或超过云模型精度，平均差距仅3.2个百分点，同时降低约800倍API成本和4倍端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人AI堆栈（如OpenClaw、Hermes Agent）几乎将所有查询（常涉及敏感本地数据）路由到云托管前沿模型，而直接用本地模型替换云模型会导致精度大幅下降（25-39个百分点）。现有堆栈将提示、工具描述、内存配置和运行时设置捆绑在特定云模型周围，仅提示可调，且最先进的提示优化器仅能缩小5个百分点的本地-云差距，不足以解决根本问题。

Method: 提出OpenJarvis架构，将个人AI系统表示为包含五个原语（智能、引擎、智能体、工具与记忆、学习）的类型规范，每个原语可独立编辑。引入LLM引导的规范搜索：在搜索时，前沿云模型对规范提出编辑建议，仅接受非退化编辑，最终生成的规范在推理时完全运行在本地设备上，实现端到端可优化和可度量。

Result: 在8个基准测试中，本地设备上运行的规范在4个上达到或超过云模型精度，平均仅落后最佳云基线3.2个百分点；边际API成本降低约800倍，端到端延迟降低4倍。

Conclusion: OpenJarvis展示了一种在不牺牲本地模型优势（隐私、低成本、低延迟）的前提下，显著缩小本地-云精度差距的有效方法，为构建安全高效的个人AI系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Personal AI stacks, like OpenClaw and Hermes Agent, are becoming central to daily work, yet they route nearly every query (often over sensitive local data) to cloud-hosted frontier models. Replacing frontier models with local models inside existing stacks does not work: swapping Claude Opus 4.6 for Qwen3.5-9B drops accuracy by 25-39 pp across personal AI tasks like PinchBench and GAIA. Existing stacks bundle agentic prompts, tool descriptions, memory configuration, and runtime settings around a specific cloud model. Only the prompts can be tuned, and state-of-the-art prompt optimizers close just 5 pp of the local-cloud gap on their own. This motivates a decomposed personal AI stack: one that exposes individual primitives which can be optimized individually or jointly to close the local-cloud gap. We present OpenJarvis, an architecture that represents a personal AI system as a typed spec over five primitives: Intelligence, Engine, Agents, Tools & Memory, and Learning. Each primitive is an independently editable field, making the stack end-to-end optimizable and measurable against accuracy, cost, and latency. Towards closing the local-cloud gap without surrendering local-model properties, OpenJarvis introduces LLM-guided spec search, a local-cloud collaboration in which frontier cloud models propose edits across the spec at search time, only non-regressing edits are accepted, and the resulting spec runs entirely on-device at inference time. With LLM-guided spec search, on-device specs match or exceed cloud accuracy on 4 of 8 benchmarks and land within 3.2 pp of the best cloud baseline on average. They also reduce marginal API cost by ~800x and end-to-end latency by 4x.

</details>


### [188] [OPTNet: Ordering Point Transformer Network for Post-disaster 3D Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17197)
*Nhut Le,Ehsan Karimi,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPTNet，通过可学习的点排序模块替代固定序列化方法，在3DAeroRelief数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于静态序列化的点云Transformer（如PTv3）使用固定顺序（Hilbert曲线/Z-order）组织点云，难以捕捉灾后场景的复杂几何结构。

Method: 提出OPTNet，核心是可学习的Point Sorter模块，通过自监督排序损失动态预测最优排列，最大化注意力机制的局部性。

Result: 在3DAeroRelief数据集上显著超越当前最优方法。

Conclusion: 动态可学习的点排序方法比固定序列化更能适应复杂几何场景，有效提升灾后点云语义分割性能。

Abstract: Post-disaster damage assessment requires rapid and accurate semantic segmentation of 3D point clouds to identify critical infrastructure such as damaged buildings and roads. Early Point Transformers (e.g., PTv1, PTv2) relied on computationally expensive neighbor searching (k-NN) and Farthest Point Sampling (FPS). To improve efficiency, recent architectures like Point Transformer V3 (PTv3) adopted static serialization methods, such as Hilbert curves or Z-order, to organize unstructured points for window-based attention. However, these fixed orderings are not optimal for capturing the complex geometry of disaster scenes. In this paper, we propose OPTNet (Ordering Point Transformer Network), which introduces a learnable Point Sorter module. OPTNet utilizes a self-supervised ordering loss to dynamically predict an optimal permutation that maximizes the locality of the attention mechanism. We evaluate our method on the 3DAeroRelief dataset, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [189] [FishBack: Pullback Fisher Geometry for Optimal Activation Steering in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17231)
*Sihan Wang,Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FishBack框架，通过使用Fisher信息度量的反推来修正语言模型激活空间中的几何结构，证明了传统欧几里得假设在steering任务中失败（GPT-2上相对谱范数偏差超过97%），并导出了最小失真方向的闭式解，无需数据驱动的几何估计。实验表明，迭代反推steering在多个概念和层上显著优于欧几里得基线方法（如CAA、ActAdd、ITI）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活steering方法普遍假设激活空间是欧几里得的，但这一假设在实际中严重不成立，导致输出失真和性能受限。本文希望建立更准确的几何框架以改善steering效果。

Method: 从softmax层的Fisher信息度量出发，通过后续层的雅可比反推，推导出闭式steering方程，识别出每个目标概念的最小失真方向。利用层间递归分解，揭示现有方法（CAA、ActAdd、ITI）隐含的特定近似度量，并通过谱诊断比率定量预测其性能差距。

Result: GPT-2实验表明，欧几里得假设下相对谱范数偏差超过97%，有效维度仅为环境空间的2–17%。迭代反推steering在三个动词形态概念和四个层上一致优于所有欧几里得基线，off-target KL降低1.3倍–2.5倍（相较于欧几里得梯度上升），相较于CAA降低1.5倍（匹配概念概率时）。
